# Abstraction layers: fractal summary and detail above and below the object

This document is task-scoped and follows [`taskPlanning/AGENT.md`](../../../../AGENT.md).

| File | Holds | Read it when |

| [`AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md`](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md) | Phase 0's worked cases, C1--C13 | Always, before arguing any row --- Phases 1 and 2 take their input from it |
| [`AGENT.abstractionLayers.discussion.planning.md`](AGENT.abstractionLayers.discussion.planning.md) | The reasoning trail, moved unchanged | Only when you need *why* a row is shaped the way it is |

**Reading map, added 2026-08-07: what's live design versus dead-hypothesis history.** [H1](#h1-single-host-objects-hosting-at-the-containing-node) (single-host objects) was tested to falsification and withdrawn the same day it's discussed below; [Proposal P1](#proposal-p1-single-host-objects--hosting-at-the-containing-node) is the proposal H1 was built from. Both are struck through and marked, not deleted --- **this initiative records supersessions rather than erasing them, so the falsification stays load-bearing evidence** --- but that means the single largest contiguous block of text in this file (Proposal P1 through "Part-mediated reference: how H1 answers its own replacement trigger," inside [Working hypotheses](#working-hypotheses)) argues for and against a hypothesis nobody is building. **If you're picking this up cold: the live design is [H3](#h3-ports-as-the-boundary-mechanism-decomposition-as-a-modellers-choice) (Working hypotheses) and [Proposal P4](#proposal-p4-settling-dataflow-and-narrative-heat) (accepted as a Prototype, iteration 1) --- read those, and the Open decisions table, before the H1/P1 block.** The H1/P1 block is dense, confident-sounding prose about a mechanism (part-mediated reference, single-host grounding) that H3 replaced; it is easy to skim, absorb a conclusion from, and not notice it was reached under a model that no longer applies. **Not relocated out of this file yet** --- flagged here instead, with the expectation of watching for actual cross-contamination as work continues rather than assuming it in advance; see [Progress](#progress) for anything that surfaces.

**Reference convention, applied throughout both files (2026-08-10): every clause reference names its construct.** **`clause N` never appears bare** --- because *clause* denotes five different things here, and the overlap is total where it matters: **H1** (1a, 1b, 2--5), **H3** (1--9), **P2** (1--3), **P5** (1--11) and the [locked frame](#locked-frame-parts-and-ports-2026-08-06) (1--3) **all define a clause 1 and a clause 3**, which between them were half of every reference in the file. *P3 and P4 define no clauses of their own*, so a reference inside those sections always points elsewhere. **Possessive and adjectival forms are equivalent** --- *H3 clause 6*, *the locked frame's clause 1*, *locked-frame clause 3*; what is required is that the construct name sit adjacent to the reference. **Do not infer a referent from the enclosing section.** It does not predict, in either direction: inside **P3** the bare references meant **H1** (P3 was written arguing about H1), and inside the **locked frame** section `clause 1b` means **H1**. Two exceptions are deliberate and should not be "fixed": a *metalinguistic* mention, where the token `clause 1` is being quoted rather than used, and a reference already qualified by adjacent prose (*H1's old clauses 4/5*).

**One durable landing has already happened, ahead of any code.** The [locked frame](#locked-frame-parts-and-ports-2026-08-06)'s two shape claims now live in [`positions/AGENT.concepts.md` --- Wholes, parts, and ports](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.concepts.md#wholes-parts-and-ports) as a **Target** mental model. That is the authority for what a whole, a part and a port *are*; this plan remains the authority for everything still open about them, and nothing has gone to `AGENT.contract.md`.

**Sibling initiative, deliberately separate:** [`AGENT.objectManipulationIterations.planning.md`](../actions/AGENT.objectManipulationIterations.planning.md) is the standing index for the **parse and reasoning pipeline** (classify -> Parse -> Plan -> Synthesize -> kernel). That ladder's rungs *consume* the world model; this initiative changes *what the world model is*. They interact --- see [Relationship to the object-manipulation ladder](#relationship-to-the-object-manipulation-ladder) --- but a rung on that ladder is the wrong container for a data-structure question that also reaches WML authoring, perception, and diegetic state.

---

## Why this initiative exists

The shipped position model has exactly one granularity: the human-convenience scale of Object and Character nodes on a host graph, related by peer-to-peer edges (`On`, `Under`, `Against`, `Custom`) within a single host. That model is a fine starting point and is not in question. What is in question is whether it can carry complexity **without becoming unworkable to reason about** --- for an LLM in particular, but the burden is not only the LLM's.

The gap is structural, not ergonomic. **Membership is the only level-crossing relation the model has, and it is flat**: a node is in a host or it is not, with no way to be in it *as part of something*. Every other relation is intra-host by construction --- [`applyStepSequenceCore`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/manipulation/kernel/applyStepSequenceCore.ts) derives a relational step's shared host live and **throws** on mismatch.

**Membership is not, however, single-host at the storage layer** --- a point that was overstated in an earlier draft of this file and is worth stating precisely, since AB-8 turns on it. See [Multi-host presence: what is actually true today](#multi-host-presence-what-is-actually-true-today) below.

So there is no way to say "these five things together are a thing," or "this thing has parts," or "this thing extends across those rooms," except by encoding it in a vocabulary that has no levels in it --- which pushes the level structure into every consumer's head, to be re-derived by inspection each time. That is the burden.

### The four seed examples

These are the origin cases, recorded verbatim in intent because the corpus in Phase 0 is built *from* them and must not lose their variety:

1. **Place setting.** Plate, cup, fork, knife, spoon `On` a table. At some point it should be possible to reason about and display "table with a place setting" rather than six nodes and five edges.
2. **Dismantled flashlight.** Improvise "parts" --- battery, bulb --- at a level *below* the object, on demand.
3. **Powered door.** A door that opens only when power is on, where power is an area-wide fact affected by repairs to a generator elsewhere. Demonstrates that abstractions likely convey **state** as well as position.
4. **Rope through several rooms.** Pulling it is mediated not by an individual room but by an Area or by an abstraction.

### They are probably not one structure

The examples separate along at least three axes, and conflating them is the main design risk this plan exists to manage:

| Axis | Seed examples | Character |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Composition (part/whole)** | 1, 2 | Level-crossing, rooted, spatially coherent. The two examples are mirror images of **one** relation --- the difference is only which level pre-existed and which was improvised into being. Place setting: members primary, whole minted. Flashlight: whole primary, parts minted. |
| **Functional aggregate carrying state** | 3 | Members deliberately **not** co-located; membership is participation in a system, not containment. Carries state; the state gates behavior elsewhere. |
| **Multi-host extent** | 4 | One thing present across several hosts --- **representable today**, contrary to an earlier draft; see [Multi-host presence](#multi-host-presence-what-is-actually-true-today). Distinguished by needing **adjacency-mediated connections** among its parts (AB-26 --- *not* a path ordering; see that row), and by making the aggregate a candidate **manipulation host** peer to Room and Character. |

**Do not assume these collapse into one `Abstraction` type, and do not assume they stay separate.** Phase 1 decides it, against the corpus. Everything downstream reads differently depending on the answer, which is exactly why it is the root question.

### Clean fractal, dirty implementation

**Named 2026-08-06, alongside the *Abstraction Fractal* candidate on AB-0.** The fractal framing promises **scale-invariance** --- the same part-of relation at every level, no privileged one --- and the corpus does not fully deliver it. That is not one problem but two, and conflating them would either discard a good model or excuse a real gap.

| | What it is | How to treat it |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Implementation dirt** | The clean model holds, and the implementation deviates from it deliberately, for efficiency: human-scale anchoring, caches, fast paths, shorthands | **Expected and legitimate.** Keep the clean fractal as the *specification of correctness*, and enumerate each deviation as an optimisation over it. A deviation that cannot be stated as "the clean answer, computed faster" is not dirt --- it is a model change wearing dirt's clothing |
| **Conceptual limit** | The clean model itself is not scale-invariant, independent of any implementation | **A real limit on the metaphor**, which the name must not paper over |
| **Scale-relative truth** *(added 2026-08-06)* | The model gives **different** answers at different levels and **both are correct** --- the coarse one is not an approximation of the fine one | **Neither dirt nor limit --- this is what scale-invariance should mean.** Requires only that the answers be *consistent*, never that they be the same. A deviation qualifies only if the coarse answer is right at the level read; if it is a lossy stand-in for a finer truth, it is dirt |

**The third row exists because the first two presumed a shape the design has since outgrown.** As originally written, the table admits only *one true answer, plus caches over it* --- so anything level-dependent had to be filed as an optimisation or as a failure of the metaphor. **[Port coarsening](#a-port-is-a-scale-boundary-not-a-relay-2026-08-06) is neither.** A reader at the room level sees "the rope is tied to the boulder"; the interior knows which end. The coarse fact is not a cheaper version of the fine one and not a break in the fractal --- it is the answer at that scale, and it is **lossless at the level read**. **This is the second time this section has been short an outcome** (the first was AB-16, where the missing answer was "no difference"), which is worth noting as a pattern: a two-column classification invites forcing, and the forced answer has been wrong both times.

**Already-known instances of each.** *Dirt:* AB-15's default binding level is exactly human-scale anchoring --- the ontology privileges no level, the **pragmatics** privilege the authored one; and the render cache is a literal cache over the clean answer, so this is not hypothetical. *Conceptual limit:* **AB-13's DAG.** Multiple parents means the structure is not a tree-like recursion, and no amount of implementation care makes it one --- that is a property of composition, not of storage. ~~**AB-16 is unclassified** and worth deciding deliberately.~~ **Classified and closed 2026-08-06: neither.** Homogeneous extent versus heterogeneous separable parts is **not a break in scale-invariance and not an implementation convenience** --- it is not a distinction the position model draws at all. See the retired **AB-16**. Kept here because the *question* was the right one to ask; the answer just came back "no difference," which this section had not offered as a third outcome and should have.

**A worked run of the test, added 2026-08-09, and the first time it has been used to *decide* something rather than to classify something already decided.** `EphemeraPositionGraphData.hostId` splits under it: as **"which node in `nodes` is the root"** it is textbook dirt --- the clean answer is derivable by traversal (the unique node with no incoming containment edge) and storing it computes that faster --- while as **"which collection this graph belongs to"** it is drift, [P2's phantom edge](#the-conceptual-error-it-starts-from-hosting-creates-a-phantom-edge), an untypeable link with no node to be an endpoint. **One field, two jobs, opposite verdicts**, which is P2's own *one construct doing two jobs* diagnosis arriving a second time one level down. **Two things this establishes about the test itself, beyond the instance.** (1) ***Deliberate* is not *dirt*.** [AB-33](#open-decisions-design--plan-only) offered the adjacency row's missing third column as *"evidence that this was a chosen shape rather than an oversight"* and treated that as settling the question. It does not: the frame's whole point is that a deviation can be perfectly deliberate and still be a model change wearing dirt's clothing. **A deliberateness argument is not a dirt argument**, and the two are easy to swap because both sound like *someone thought about this*. (2) **The unit of the test is the job, not the construct.** Asking "is `hostId` dirt" has no answer; asking it of each job the field does has two, and they differ. **Apply the test per job wherever a construct is suspected of doing more than one.**

**Why the split earns its keep:** it lets the clean model be held as correctness while the implementation is permitted to deviate, with every deviation named as an optimisation rather than quietly absorbed into the model. The failure mode it guards against is the opposite --- efficiency shortcuts accumulating until nobody can say what the model *was*, which is the state the position lane's four routes reached with single-host (see [C2](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c2-the-rope-in-two-rooms-refused-at-the-gate)).

### Fiction is a fault-tolerant medium --- and what that does not excuse (2026-08-09)

**Recorded from conversation as a standing frame, not as a clause inside the proposal that prompted it.** It already has three consumers --- [coarsening](#clean-fractal-dirty-implementation), [P4](#two-defects-inherited-with-the-model)'s glitch policy (**PS-5** offers *accept transient wrong values* as a live option), and [P5](#licence-transfer-import-is-a-copy-and-provenance-is-deliberately-not-traced-2026-08-09)'s no-cascade decision --- which is the test for a frame rather than a local argument.

**The claim.** Reasoning over coarse summaries can produce errors and that is accepted. The same tolerance applies more broadly: **some player-visible contradiction is fine.** We are telling a story, not assembling a logical proof. This is **a property of the medium, not a concession extracted from it** --- audiences of long-form fiction do active repair work, tolerate continuity errors in enormous quantity, and frequently invent explanations more interesting than the consistency would have been. A tabletop GM contradicting themselves and moving on is not failing at the job; it is the job.

**And the alternatives are worse, which is the argument that actually decides it.** Guaranteeing coherence requires one of: refusing to improvise (generic worlds), tracing every derivation (unscalable --- the cost P5 declined), or blocking reads until everything settles (unresponsive). **Each costs more than an occasional napkin**, and the first costs the thing this initiative exists to provide.

#### What a *fault* even is, given the medium (2026-08-09)

**The paragraphs above still smuggle in a software instinct, and this corrects it.** A fault here is **not** *the system produced the wrong fiction* --- **there is no wrong fiction.** Any napkin is as good as any other napkin; no output is incorrect by failing to match a truth that existed before someone wrote it. **A fault is something that strains the integrity of the story.**

**Two consequences, and the second is why this is worth restating rather than filing as philosophy.**

1. **Accuracy is the wrong standard, so validating against truth is the wrong mechanism.** There is nothing to validate against. What *can* be checked is **consistency with what the fiction has already committed to** --- a different and far smaller obligation than correctness, and one that suggests different machinery.
2. **Strain is a matter of degree, and it tracks *salience*.** This is the discerning lens the fault-tolerance argument was missing. If nobody mentioned the napkins, contradicting them costs nothing. If the scene was *about* the tavern's poverty, the same contradiction is expensive. **Same fact, same contradiction, completely different cost.**

~~**And nothing structural distinguishes the two cases**, which is exactly why it cannot be read off the graph.~~ **Withdrawn 2026-08-09, challenged the same day it was written, and the correction is more useful than the claim.** **A scene about the tavern's poverty goes hand in hand with minting licence restrictions** --- under [P5](#proposal-p5-improvisational-licence-as-a-first-class-graph-item), poverty *is* the closing of a licence to improvise abundance. The incidental case has no record at all, only the unbounded default. **So salience does leave structural traces, and the primary trace is licence being *touched*.**

**Stated as a correlate rather than an identity, because it is lossy in both directions:**

| | Example | Why it breaks |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Salient, licence untouched** | A scene about the tavern's *warmth and generosity* | Contradicting it (a surly innkeeper) strains badly with no narrowing to detect. **Partly repaired by reading licence *change* rather than *narrowing*** --- generosity plausibly **widens** --- but tone that changes no licence at all still exists |
| **Licence touched, not salient** | [C19](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c19-filling-out-the-place-setting-and-the-tavern-that-ran-out-of-napkins) beat 4's throwaway stock question | Narrows the tavern's licence and matters to nobody. A detector keying on narrowing alone would over-report |

**Three candidate structural signals, none assessed:** whether licence was touched at all; whether a change belongs to a **cluster** of related changes (poverty narrows napkins *and* ale *and* linens; an incidental answer narrows one), which is the most promising and the cheapest to compute; and **heat at the moment of establishment**, recorded on the change.

**The consequence worth chasing, flagged as speculative:** if a place's *character* --- poor, lavish, decayed, orderly --- is represented largely as the shape of its licence, then **licence is the generative half of setting character**: positive detail is what is there, licence is what the place will produce when asked. That would make P5 considerably more central than *a guard on improvisation*, and it is not what the proposal currently claims. **Not asserted, and it should be tested before P5 is leaned on for it.**

**So the damping rule sharpens from *damp faults* to *damp faults in proportion to salience*.** Global consistency is not the target and never was; consistency **where the story has invested** is. **And per the correction above, "where the story has invested" is partly readable from where licence has been touched** --- which makes the rule cheaper to act on than a purely judgment-based salience would be.

**And that is a third job for [narrative heat](#narrative-heat-the-optimization-that-is-probably-not-only-an-optimization).** Salience is close to what heat already measures --- what the fiction and its players are attending to. **[PS-4](#open-questions-p4-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused) asks whether heat is one artifact or two; this makes it potentially three**, and that row's own warning applies unchanged: **do not assume the cheap, approximate scheduling heat can carry a correctness-adjacent consumer.** Recorded as feeding PS-4, not as answering it.

#### The line this produces, and a terminology collision to fix rather than absorb

***Integrity* is now doing two jobs in this document, and they are not the same job.** The user-facing sense above --- *the integrity of the story* --- is the broad one and the more natural English. The sense used elsewhere in this plan is structural. **Qualify both rather than letting context disambiguate**, since a reader hitting the bare word will pick whichever meaning their background supplies:

| Term | What it covers | Status |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Narrative integrity** | **Whether participant contributions are reincorporated** --- see the definition below, which narrows this considerably from *whether the story holds together* | **The measure.** This is what a fault strains, and it is a matter of degree |
| **Structural well-formedness** | One referent per thing, membership matching adjacency, no orphans, no double-mints, no dangling edges | **Non-negotiable**, and see below for why the unified measure does not soften it |
| **Coherence** | Whether the established facts contradict each other | **One component of narrative integrity, and usually the cheapest.** Not a synonym for it |

**Why structural well-formedness stays non-negotiable under a single graded measure.** A dangling edge nobody traverses strains nothing *today* --- so on the measure alone it would be tolerable. It is not, because **structural defects are latent strain: they cash out later, unpredictably, and usually as the world failing to behave like a world**, which is the most expensive kind. Two place settings where there was one is not a charming inconsistency; it is the reality illusion breaking, and everything else rests on that illusion. **Treat structural defects as high-strain-with-a-delay rather than as a separate category exempt from the measure.**

**P5's no-cascade decision spends *coherence* and preserves *structural well-formedness* entirely, which is exactly why it is safe** --- the napkin is one napkin, correctly keyed and correctly placed; only its provenance sits in tension with a fact about the storeroom that no scene had leaned on.

#### Narrative integrity is in service to reincorporation (2026-08-09)

**A narrower definition than *whether the story holds together*, and it is the one to build against.** In a story game, narrative integrity serves the **reincorporation of participant contributions** --- the improv discipline (Keith Johnstone, *Impro*) of taking what a participant introduced and bringing it back so that having contributed **mattered**. **Note the lineage:** almost all of this plan's prior art is computational --- dataflow, truth maintenance, importance sampling, capability attenuation. **This is the first load-bearing frame taken from theatre craft**, which for a fiction system is arguably where it should have come from first.

**What the narrowing buys, on the case that motivated it.** A tavern described as well-stocked one day and poorly stocked the next is, technically, a major consistency violation. **In story-gaming terms it is close to unimportant** --- if nobody put anything into either state, no contribution is betrayed by either. **The violation that matters is when the contradicted state was (a) *authored* as well-stocked, or (b) *worked for in play* by players who hauled supplies in.** Same contradiction, and the difference is entirely who invested in it.

**So this supersedes salience as the criterion, and demotes it to a proxy.** **Salience measures attention; contribution measures investment**, and investment is what integrity protects. The two usually agree and come apart in both directions: a player's throwaway remark that their character hates cats is low-salience and *high*-value to reincorporate, while a system-improvised atmospheric detail a scene happened to dwell on is high-salience and nobody's contribution.

**And it replaces the structural signal proposed above with a better one.** [The licence-clustering heuristic](#what-a-fault-even-is-given-the-medium-2026-08-09) was a guess at salience. **Contribution has a structural trace that already exists and is exact rather than heuristic:** the three-way split of **authored** (assets and WML, recorded in the participation stack), **player-driven** (actions routed through the manipulation kernel, producing committed step sequences and events), and **system-improvised** (`ASSET#IMPROVISATION`). **That split is already in storage**, and it maps directly onto how much a contradiction costs. Prefer it to clustering.

**Which makes this the *third* dent in one claim, and three is enough to restate rather than patch.** AB-14 holds that *"`ASSET#IMPROVISATION` is a merge layer, so provenance is invisible downstream."* [C17](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c17-the-professional-kitchen-the-chefs-knife-and-the-stand-mixer-nobody-asked-about) Finding 7 dented it, [AB-45](#open-decisions-design--plan-only) dented it again, and now the weighting of narrative integrity depends on it. **The accurate statement is narrower: provenance is invisible in the *merged view*, and fully recoverable from storage.** Those were being conflated, and only the first was ever argued.

**The division of labour this produces is cleaner than either rule alone.** Reincorporation covers authored and player commitments, tone and genre (the author contributed those too), and stakes. **Structural well-formedness covers the reality illusion** --- two place settings where there was one is not best described as a contribution violation. **Together they cover the ground; neither does alone.**

**What it argues for, at some future point: narrative weight carried alongside the permissive structures.** A licence record would carry not only *what* was established but **how much investment stands behind it** --- authored, worked-for-in-play, or improvised in passing. That gives the damping rule a real dial: protect high-weight commitments, let low-weight ones drift. **And it composes with P5's no-cascade decision rather than fighting it, for a specific reason: weight is a *value carried on the record*, not a pointer to its source.** Copy, not reference --- the same discipline [licence transfer](#licence-transfer-import-is-a-copy-and-provenance-is-deliberately-not-traced-2026-08-09) already adopted. Tracked as **AB-46**.

**Recorded with its negative statement, on [AB-0](#open-decisions-design--plan-only)'s precedent**, because *fiction is fault-tolerant* is a sentence that will be quoted to excuse real defects:

0. **"There is no wrong fiction" does not mean anything goes.** It **relocates** the standard, it does not remove it: the measure becomes *strain on narrative integrity* rather than *accuracy against a truth*. A horror scene interrupted by a knock-knock joke is not defensible on the grounds that no fiction is wrong --- it is high strain, and the framing says so. **This clause exists because *no wrong fiction* is quotable in exactly the way the section title is**, and both need their limits attached.
1. **It does not excuse structural defects.** Two place settings where there should be one is not a charming inconsistency; it is the reality illusion breaking, and AB-21 exists for it. See *latent strain* above.
2. **It does not excuse a contradiction a player *acted on*.** Reasoning *there are no napkins, so I will use my handkerchief* and then finding a napkin is **agency undermined**, not flavour. **This is why re-open triggers belong at *consequence*, not at visibility** --- a contradiction nobody built on is cheap; one that invalidated a decision is not.
3. **It does not obviously extend to LLM context, and that is open rather than settled.** The tolerant audience is a *reader*. A reasoning step is a **second consumer with different tolerance**, and contradictory context does not produce a charming inconsistency --- it may produce a confidently wrong plan. How much this matters is unmeasured; it should not be assumed benign because the human half is.

**The general rule for triggers, applied to P5 and to be applied to PS-5:** set them at **consequence**, not at detectability. Theoretical incoherence, and even noticed incoherence, is inside the tolerance. Incoherence that broke something is not.

#### The feedback regime is the thing to measure, and it decides which solutions to look for (2026-08-09)

**Stated in conversation as a principle for the system generally, not only for this plan**, and it is the sharper half of the section above. Point 3 above says LLM tolerance is unmeasured. **The measurement that matters is not a rate --- it is a *regime*.**

| Regime | Behaviour | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Negative feedback / damping** | Minor faults tend to be corrected, absorbed, or papered over as the story continues | **Fine.** Fault tolerance is real and the medium does the work |
| **Positive feedback / amplifying** | Faults spawn progressively larger and more consequential faults | **Problematic**, and no amount of per-fault tolerance saves it |

**The design principle that follows, and it is the load-bearing sentence:** **look for solutions that promote the damping regime, rather than solutions that leave a positive feedback cycle in place and try to prevent error from ever occurring.** Prevention is the instinctive move and it is the wrong tool here --- an LLM in the loop *will* produce mildly incoherent details, and a system built to stop that costs the improvisation that is the point.

**This maps onto the line above, and the mapping is the practical form of the rule:**

- **Structural well-formedness -> prevention.** Gates, asserts, invariants. `removeObject`'s assert-and-throw, classify-before-legal, the compile gates. **Correct discipline, keep it** --- and note prevention is affordable *there* precisely because well-formedness has a definition to check against, which narrative integrity does not.
- **Coherence, and narrative integrity generally -> damping.** Prevention is unavailable (nothing to validate against) and unaffordable; the question is whether faults attenuate as they travel, **weighted by salience**.

**What damps, including two arguments for existing mechanisms that had not been made:**

1. **Narration entrenches, and entrenchment is *stabilizing* here.** Once a detail is narrated it is canon, and both the player and later reasoning build on it. In a database an error becoming permanent is alarming; **in fiction it converts a fault into shared truth and the divergence stops.** This is the *papering over* half, and it is a mechanism rather than a hope.
2. **Coarsening is a low-pass filter.** A fault in fine detail may simply not survive summarization to the next step. **[The port as summarization seam](#a-third-argument-on-a-third-axis-the-port-is-the-summarization-seam-2026-08-07) gains a fourth argument on a fourth axis: it is a fault-damping seam.** **With a real caveat:** [C12 Finding 3](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#finding-3-coarsening-is-truth-preserving-on-containment-and-truth-destroying-on-causality) found coarsening *invents* conclusions on causality, so it damps detail-faults and can amplify causal ones. Not uniformly a filter.
3. **Bounded context is fault isolation.** This initiative exists to hand a reasoning step the right-sized packet. A fault outside the packet cannot influence it. **The scalability thesis and the stability thesis are the same mechanism, which is a second payoff nobody was charging for.**
4. **Players repair.** They notice, ask, object, and incorporate --- free correction, concentrated exactly where attention is, which is also where [heat](#narrative-heat-the-optimization-that-is-probably-not-only-an-optimization) says consequence concentrates.

**What amplifies --- and the first one is this architecture's own commitment, which is why it is listed first:**

1. **Summaries of summaries.** The fractal asserts **no privileged level**, so arbitrary-depth summarization chains are structurally normal --- [C11](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c11-the-rube-goldberg-machine)'s four-level chain is the shape. Each hop is lossy and occasionally inventive, and drift compounds across hops. **This is the concrete positive-feedback risk the Abstraction Fractal buys along with its scale-invariance**, and it belongs in AB-0's departures list rather than being discovered later.
2. **Causal cascades with cycles.** [P4](#two-defects-inherited-with-the-model) already flags oscillation on cycles as a known cost. **A fault entering a cycle is not merely oscillation --- it is amplification**, and the guard P4 wants for termination is not the same guard fault-amplification needs.
3. **Licence widened on improvised grounds.** [C19](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c19-filling-out-the-place-setting-and-the-tavern-that-ran-out-of-napkins)'s import supplies an intensional description that unlocks further improvisation. **If the description supplying it was itself improvised, invention compounds** --- recorded honestly because it lands on a mechanism designed the same day, and because P5's no-cascade decision means nothing walks back an over-wide licence.

**Not a sixth axis, and the distinction is not pedantic.** [The five axes](#the-five-axes-what-the-graphs-flexibility-is-in-service-to) ask *what does the graph buy, and for whom*. This asks *how does a proposed mechanism behave under perturbation* --- a **dynamics** question about a design, not a value it serves. [State was declined as a sixth axis](#considered-and-declined-state-as-a-sixth-axis-2026-08-07) for a different reason; this is declined because it is a different **kind** of question, and folding it in would make the axis tally incoherent.

**What to instrument, since this is a measurement claim.** [P4 iteration 1](#what-p4-being-load-bearing-here-means-2026-08-07) is already committed to being *built to measure* --- fan-out, diamond frequency, settle time. **Add a fourth: fault propagation.** Direct measurement is hard; the proxies are not. How often does a later reasoning step **contradict** an earlier established fact? How deep do summarization chains actually run in play? Does contradiction frequency **rise** with world age --- which is the regime question stated as something countable?

**A graduation candidate, deliberately not graduated.** This is a claim about how to build the system generally, not about position graphs, so it will outlive this initiative. **Nothing goes to a durable doc until it ships** ([`taskPlanning/AGENT.md`](../../../../AGENT.md)), and nothing here is built --- so it stays, flagged, and should be revisited for graduation when P4 iteration 1 returns its first measurements.

### Multi-host presence: what is actually true today

Verified against the code 2026-08-05, because the rope case (AB-8) had been written up as breaking a hard invariant, and that is not accurate. **Multi-room object presence is representable today with no schema change.** What stands in the rope's way is narrower and more specific.

| Layer | Reality |
| --- | --- |
| **Adjacency storage** | Natively multi-host. `getMembershipContainers` returns an **array**; adjacency rows are `(OBJECT#, POSITION#ROOM#...)` with nothing unique-constraining them. `MembershipDiff.froms` is plural and [`AGENT.contract.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.contract.md) explicitly permits `froms.length > 1`. |
| **Membership compile gate** | **The hardest pressure, and the earliest.** Corrected 2026-08-05 while mining C2 --- an earlier draft named the end-state planner as "the" pressure. [`complexityPreGates.ts:29`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/actions/enrich/objectManipulation/complexityPreGates.ts) returns `complexityClass: 'multiPresent'` for `containers.length > 1`, and `preGateOutcomeToTerminalError` maps it straight to player-facing error copy. A multi-present object is **refused before planning is reached**. |
| **End-state planner** | A second, softer pressure. [`computeEndStateRoomDiff`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/manipulation/adapters/computeEndStateRoomDiff.ts) computes `froms = priorContainers.filter(!== to)`, so ordinary place / spawn / remove scrub every other room as **normal operation**, not as repair. A planner behavior, not an invariant. |
| **Relational compile** | A third, and different again: [`compileRelationalFromSkeleton.ts:168`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/actions/enrich/objectManipulation/compileRelationalFromSkeleton.ts) populates `hostByObjectId` only `if (containers.length === 1)`, so a multi-present object is **silently hostless** to Expansion rather than refused. |
| **Cross-host move route** | Bypasses the gate *and* the planner --- `executeObjectMove` grounds through the Synthesize executor from a host pair. So the constraint is **not one rule applied unevenly across routes; it is three different rules on three routes.** See [C2](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c2-the-rope-in-two-rooms-refused-at-the-gate) for the full table. |
| **Kernel step shape** | `MutationKernelTransferStep` has `fromHostIds: ReadonlySet<...>` but a **singular `toHostId`**. There is no add-to-many primitive. Three-room presence needs three pure-add steps or a widened field. |
| **Relational edges** | Strictly intra-host regardless. A rope in room A cannot relate to a bollard in room B under any current shape. |
| **Presentation** | Affordance compose reads `graph.objectIds` per room, so a multi-room rope renders as a **whole object in each room** with no notion of partial presence. Likely the first thing a player would notice. |
| **Drift repair** | [`repairObjectPlacementDrift`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/membership/repairObjectPlacementDrift.ts) **has no production caller** --- only its own test. The `Object Placement Drift Finding` sweep that would feed it is deferred and does not exist in [`lambda/diagnostics/`](../../../../../lambda/diagnostics/). Nothing is eliminating multi-room presence at runtime. |

**A four-way disagreement to resolve deliberately, not silently** (three when first written; the compile gate is the fourth, added 2026-08-05). The durable docs currently assert the opposite of the intended design: [`AGENT.contract.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.contract.md) says "**Steady state:** at most one room per `OBJECT#`; multi-room adjacency is drift," and [`AGENT.concepts.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.concepts.md) says "Objects follow the same steady-state rule (nodes only)." **Storage** permits multi-host while the **membership compile gate** refuses it outright and the **relational** route silently ignores it; the stated design (2026-08-05) is that a rope should eventually be present in several rooms. Either the clause was scoped to **characters** and over-generalized to objects when object membership shipped, or the design moved and the docs did not. **Fix the docs as part of Phase 1**, whichever way it resolves --- an unwired repairer plus a contract clause forbidding what the design intends is exactly the kind of stale normative text that gets obeyed by a future reader who has no reason to doubt it.

### Improvisation is the objects lane's mechanism, generalized

Settled 2026-08-05, and it changes what several rows cost. **`ASSET#IMPROVISATION` is not a flag on a special kind of object --- it is an asset layer in the participation stack.** Improvised bodies merge through composite `ComponentData` like any other layer, and spawn-time `DEFAULT` situation prose deliberately routes objects through the **same** `ensureAuthoredCatalog` render path Feature / Knowledge / Character use rather than a bespoke stub. So the mechanism's actual purpose is already the general one this initiative needs: **mint a component nobody authored, such that nothing downstream can tell the difference.** What it lacked was a name for that purpose. Full detail: [`objects/AGENT.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/objects/AGENT.md).

**What arrives already built:** the four-way split (merge body / play meta / semantic embedding / placement), a two-step existence-then-placement sequence, the **S1** compensating delete, the orphan sweep and its delete-only repair, and a best-effort **LLM-in-the-loop discipline** (Bedrock embeddings and enrich-generated prose, both failure-tolerant and non-blocking) that AB-18's cost and latency policy should start from rather than re-derive.

**This sharpens AB-12 from a philosophical question into a cost question.** If Objects are already abstractions at every level, minting a battery or a place setting **is** minting an `OBJECT#` --- the lane needs no generalization at all, only use. The Room / Feature half is different in kind: every row key, module, and type in that lane is `OBJECT#`-shaped. The two bets therefore differ by roughly *no new storage* versus *a new storage shape*, which is far more decidable than the ontology argument.

**Three gaps, all bounded, all real:**

| Gap | Detail |
| --- | --- |
| **No composition relation** | Improvisation mints a **free-standing** object and places it in a room. A *part* needs the level-crossing edge as well --- so spawn+place becomes spawn+place+compose, or the compose **is** the place if AB-4 resolves part-of as a host relationship. Either way the S1 compensating-delete story needs revisiting, since it currently compensates a two-step sequence. |
| **Recognition fits less cleanly than Improvisation** | Improvisation creates a thing from nothing, so its existence rows are the whole story. Recognition creates an aggregate **over things that already exist**: its body is nearly empty and its **member set is the content**. The lane has no concept of a component whose identity is a set of other components. |
| **`EMBEDDING#IMPROMPTU` is `shortName`-derived** | Reasonable for a battery, questionable for "the place setting on the table." Whether an aggregate gets a semantic vector, and over what text, is undecided. |

**Naming consequence --- ~~carried as AB-0's concrete form~~ now its own row, **AB-38** (split out 2026-08-07 when AB-0's naming half locked).** If Improvisation names the *mechanism*, then Improvisation and Recognition are **not peers**: Recognition is a trigger that invokes the mechanism, and operational necessity is the other trigger. The word is currently doing both jobs, which is the same conflation AB-14 was restructured to remove. Two ways out, unresolved: **(a)** Improvisation and Recognition stay the two triggers and the shared mechanism takes its own name; **(b)** Improvisation names the mechanism and the downward trigger is renamed.

**One speculative consequence worth keeping.** Because `ASSET#IMPROVISATION` is a *layer*, an improvised thing can later be **authored over**. A recognized snare trap could graduate into canon --- a real partial answer to AB-19's cost objection that an authored pattern vocabulary limits players to what someone anticipated.

### Coyote as prior art: bounded LLM reasoning that is not bounded structurally

Identified 2026-08-05 as the **existing first-iteration attempt** at reasoning about player-assembled contraptions --- deliberately flawed and ad hoc, and the closest thing to a working answer for AB-19. Read [`coyoteGame/AGENT.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/coyoteGame/AGENT.md), [`coyoteGame/AGENT.tropes.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/coyoteGame/AGENT.tropes.md), and [`coyotePlanAffinities.ts`](../../../../../packages/mtw-interfaces/ts/coyotePlanAffinities.ts) before designing anything in this area.

**What it does, structurally:** a `CoyoteStagedObject` snapshot assembled from `positionGraph` + `Meta::Object` + the improvisation pair, grouped by room; each object carrying `tropeAffinities` (roles with graded `aptness`, plus `environmentAffordances` / `affordancesProvided`) written at **Acme enrich time**; then a multi-hop LLM pipeline (candidates -> planSelect -> narrativeBeats -> outcome) with structured parse between hops and an internal-only `gimmick` narrative spine.

**What generalizes --- keep these:**

| Pattern | Why it matters here |
| --- | --- |
| **Annotate at mint time, not at reasoning time** | One Bedrock call at object creation writes durable structure; the reasoning step then *consumes* it. This is the direct answer to AB-18's cost concern and the reason AB-22 exists. |
| **Closed structural vocabulary, open narrative content** | Roles and tropes are enumerable; `gimmick` is deliberately free text with no closed enum. Matches the standing rule that only genuinely enumerable vocabulary should be hardcoded. |
| **Graded `aptness`, not booleans** | Affordance is a matter of degree, and downstream hops can rank rather than filter. |
| **Multi-hop with structured parse between hops** | Each hop's output is validated JSON, so a later hop reasons over structure rather than prose. |
| **Internal-only reasoning handoff** | `gimmick` never reaches the client. Reasoning scaffolding and player-facing narration are separate artifacts. |

**What is ad hoc --- and it is precisely the gap this initiative would fill:**

- **There is no persistent assembly-level object anywhere in it.** Every prediction reconstructs "what did the player build" from a **flat bag** of staged objects plus per-object role affinities. The contraption is never a thing.
- **Context is scoped to rooms, not to the contraption** (`loadCoyoteRoomObjectsByRoom`, across all Coyote game rooms). The LLM receives everything staged, relevant or not.
- **Therefore its boundedness is circumstantial, not structural.** It comes from the world being tiny and the genre closed --- six tropes, a hardcoded `boulder | cactus | ...` affordance enum, a handful of rooms. Remove any of those and nothing bounds the context.
- **The seam-label hack** (prompt labels vs. canonical `EphemeraRoomId`, an explicitly one-way contract carrying a six-item warning list about reversing it) reads as a symptom of the same cause: prompt context assembled ad hoc rather than projected from a real structure.

**The load-bearing conclusion:** an abstraction layer would supply the boundedness **structurally** --- the contraption is a named subgraph, and that subgraph *is* the context. Coyote answers the annotation question per-object and the scope question per-room; the missing piece is per-**assembly**.

### `thinking` as the scheduling substrate: shipped ledger, unbuilt queue

Identified 2026-08-05 as the intended home for deferred recognition, and **verified against code** because the distinction between what exists and what does not is load-bearing. Read [`thinking/AGENT.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/thinking/AGENT.md).

**Shipped and reusable:** durable `Meta::Schedule` rows on a `TASK#${workItemId}` partition, a status vocabulary of `scheduled | claimed | cancelled | completed`, job bootstrap and adjacency, rollup to `Job Completed`, client dashboards, and `fetchThinkingResult` lookup by `workItemId`. The retention policy already anticipates this exact use: **"in-flight rows --- `scheduled` / `claimed` schedules ... do not set `deleteAt`, so *opportunistically queued work* is not TTL'd while waiting."**

**Not shipped --- and this is the actual work:** **nothing writes `claimed`.** Only `scheduled`, `completed`, and `cancelled` have producers; `claimed` appears in the type union and in `persistThinkingJobError`'s `IN_FLIGHT_SCHEDULE_STATUSES` for cancellation, with **no claimer anywhere**. Every thinking job today is bootstrapped at the start of a synchronous pipeline (Coyote hypothesis, Acme enrich) and completed within that same Lambda invocation. **The schedule rows are observability records of work already happening, not a queue anything pulls from.** A worker, deferred dispatch, and promotion-on-demand all remain to be built.

**One guarantee that comes free, and it closes AB-18's convergence requirement.** `Meta::Result` is written with `ephemeraDB.nonCollidingPutItem` --- conditional on `attribute_not_exists`, **first write wins**, duplicate finalize returns `alreadyFinalized`. That is exactly what "the deferred pass and the promoted foreground path must not produce two snares" needs, already shipped and already retry-safe. Two independently-derived requirements (AB-18's convergence, and promotion-on-demand from the thinking side) landing on one existing mechanism is evidence the framing is sound.

---

## The five axes: what the graph's flexibility is in service to

**Framed 2026-08-07.** The graph model's flexibility is not a single virtue. It buys **five different things for five different audiences**, and a proposal can be excellent at one while indifferent or actively bad at another. **This section is the evaluation frame the rest of the plan should argue in**, and it exists because the plan had the negative half of it for two days and could not act on it.

### Why it was needed: a correct diagnosis with no positive half

On 2026-08-05 [the falsification criteria](#what-counts-as-a-falsification-case-learned-the-hard-way-2026-08-05) worked out **why "can it represent X" keeps passing**: any sufficiently expressive graph can express almost any situation, so fictional complexity is not structural difficulty. That was right, and it was **purely negative** --- it said what not to measure and named nothing to measure instead. The result was predictable in hindsight: cases kept being built, kept being hard, and kept discriminating nothing.

**The corpus already sorted itself along these axes before they were named.** The cases that sailed through --- [C5](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c5-the-chain-mounted-at-both-ends), [C6](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c6-the-rope-the-candle-and-the-impromptu-timer), the latter adding **zero** `H1-dependent` rows --- are the ones whose difficulty was positional. The ones that bit hardest --- [C3](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c3-the-snare-trap-coyote-cannot-name), [C8](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c8-the-flashlight-that-vanished-when-someone-looked-at-it), [C10](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c10-the-moonbase-computer-system) --- are reasoning and apprehension cases. The evidence for this frame is the corpus's own track record, which is why the [axis tally](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#axis-tally-c1--c21) was retro-fitted in the same pass rather than left for new cases.

### The five

**They do not all do the same job to a proposal**, and treating them as five interchangeable scores is the failure mode this table is arranged to prevent.

| Axis | Audience | Function | Wants |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Positioning** | the world | **Filters** --- eliminates crude options, then goes quiet | Only that the arrangement be recordable |
| **Mutation** | the kernel | **Vetoes** --- survivable or not; no ranking | One-pass footprint, no re-entry, enforceable locally |
| **Apprehension** | the player | **Ranks** | To be **lossy** --- the place setting hides the smaller fork |
| **Reasoning** | the LLM | **Ranks** | **No silent omission** (corrected 2026-08-07 --- see below; first stated as "lossless", which was too strong) |
| **Authoring** | the author | **Ranks** | Sparse, deepenable, and provenance-tagged |

**Positioning is a filter, not a weak ranking, and the distinction matters.** It fires hard exactly once: [C7](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c7-ariadnes-thread) eliminated the one-node-no-ports framing outright, because a single node genuinely cannot record *where along* a thread anything attaches. Then it goes quiet. Both of the Ariadne representations now under discussion --- twenty flat spans with connective topology, versus a nested multi-room `thread-span` whole --- clear the filter identically, and positioning has nothing further to say about them. **A design argued only on positioning becomes unfalsifiable after the first round**, which is the state this plan spent two days in. Even a flat, non-hierarchical part-by-part graph records positioning correctly; physics engines run exactly that way.

**Two clarifications, added 2026-08-07 after AB-1 resolved.**

1. **The axis covers arrangement *and* causal structure**, despite its name. Once **AB-1** resolves as one substrate, "can this be recorded at all" is one question asked of `part-of`, `powers`, and everything else alike. **The name is kept rather than widened** because it matches the shipped vocabulary (`positionGraph`, `dataSource/positions`) --- a mismatch that is now a real problem in its own right, tracked at [the naming consequence](#the-relation-kind-register) below.
2. **"Fires exactly once" is per relation kind, not globally.** C7 fired it for extent; [C12](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c12-the-moonbase-power-system-and-the-cut-cable) fired it again for causality, eliminating "no carrier anywhere." **Expect one firing per kind introduced**, and expect the axis to go quiet again immediately afterwards.

**Mutation is a veto, and folding it into positioning is the specific mistake this axis exists to name.** Positioning is about *recording* an arrangement; mutation is about *transitioning* it, and nothing in the other four touches that. `computeStepSequenceFootprint` runs **once**, up front, and `MultiKeyUpdate` cannot be re-entered --- a constraint that has killed or re-priced more proposals here than apprehension has: PQ-7's enforceability charge, H1 clause 5, [rollback trigger 3](#what-counts-as-a-falsification-case-learned-the-hard-way-2026-08-05), and [C9](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c9-coiling-the-rope-back-in)'s one-record-inside / two-at-a-boundary costing. **It is not a benefit flexibility delivers; it is a constraint flexibility must survive**, which is why it vetoes rather than ranks.

**Authoring is a peer in rank and not in kind.** Positioning is the substrate and apprehension and reasoning are two consumers of it; authoring is neither. It is about **provenance and what a human gets to not write** --- a coarse authored setting ("a kitchen with all the expected fixings") that becomes deep in practice when someone asks whether there is a chef's knife. Its requirement therefore cuts across the other three: an improvised knife must be positionally coherent, apprehensible, reasonable-about, **and** distinguishable from an authored one, because asset re-merge can clobber it. That last clause is AB-29's play-vs-asset bifurcation arriving as a first-class goal instead of a caveat.

### The conflict that makes this a tool rather than a scorecard

**A benefit list with no conflicts in it is decoration** --- every proposal claims all five and nothing is decided. This one has a real one, and it is between the two axes that both look like "present the right amount of information":

> **Apprehension is lossy by design. Reasoning is lossless by design.**

The place setting is better UI *because* it hides the fork and the spoon. The rope's connective topology living inside `OBJECT#ROPE` is a win *because* nothing was dropped. **These are not two calibrations of one mechanism** --- the same abstraction must conceal its members from the player's description and expose them to the LLM asked whether there is a knife to grab.

This is [scale-relative truth](#clean-fractal-dirty-implementation) with the audience made explicit, and it retro-justifies that category: the reason both answers can be correct is that they are answers to **different consumers**, not two resolutions of one query.

#### Correction, same day: reasoning wants *no silent omission*, not losslessness

**"Lossless by design" was stated hours earlier and is too strong.** An LLM does not need every fact in the interior; it needs a guarantee that **nothing relevant was dropped without saying so**. The property, stated properly:

> **A summary may omit detail. It may not omit the *existence* of a fact, and what it omits must be retrievable.**

That is much weaker than losslessness and much more achievable --- and the [ports' summarization seam](#the-strongest-argument-for-ports-is-not-the-traversal-one) is a mechanism that delivers exactly it. Recorded as a correction rather than an edit because the stronger claim was already being reasoned from.

#### And the conflict is narrower than first stated

**Apprehension and reasoning agree about *scale* and conflict about *omission of members*.** Both want a thread named as a thread at whole level; the conflict fires only when the question needs member-level detail --- the place setting must hide the fork from the player and expose it to an LLM asked whether there is a knife to grab.

**Which means a candidate resolution now exists**, where this section originally said the frame names the conflict and stops. If a whole-level view can report *contains cutlery, one knife-kind item* without enumerating, reasoning is served and apprehension is not compromised. **Recorded as a candidate on one worked case, not as a resolution** --- and the "does not rank the axes" caution below stands unchanged, since a mechanism that sometimes dissolves a conflict is not an ordering.

### What the conflict catches immediately

**Coarsening's losslessness claim is audience-relative and was stated unqualified.** [A port is a scale boundary, not a relay](#a-port-is-a-scale-boundary-not-a-relay-2026-08-06) concludes that coarsening is *"lossless at the level being read"* --- `PowerCord -ThreadsInto-> FLASHLIGHT:1` degrading to `...-> FLASHLIGHT` reads unchanged.

**True for apprehension. Not true for reasoning.** An LLM asked whether the strap can be untied, or what the cord is soldered to, has lost exactly the fact it needs. The coarsening default is an **apprehension mechanism**, and applying it on the reasoning path is a defect rather than a graceful degradation.

**This is a live defect in something the lock treats as settled-with-a-lower-bar**, and it is recorded here rather than acted on: the fix is plausibly just *coarsening is a read-side resolution rule for description, and the reasoning path opens the bag* --- which costs a hop on a path that already expects to pay for detail. **It does not re-open a locked clause** (coarsening is calibration, explicitly on the not-locked list) and it should not be settled by this section, which is a frame rather than a decision.

### How to use it

1. **Restate the falsification criterion.** "Discriminating" now means **discriminating on a named axis**. A case where two proposals differ is not yet a test; a case where they differ *and the difference is visible to a named audience* is. This subsumes the [faithfulness clause](#what-counts-as-a-falsification-case-learned-the-hard-way-2026-08-05) added after C7 rather than replacing it.
2. **Tag every case with its axes.** Done for C1--C11 in the [axis tally](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#axis-tally-c1--c21). The tally's first result is already the sharpest thing in it --- see below.
3. **When a row will not resolve, check whether it is being argued on the wrong axis.** H1 clause 1b is the worked example: it is **not a positional question**, both framings record the world correctly, and two days were spent trading positional arguments about it.

### What the tally immediately produced

**No case in the corpus is primary on authoring.** Eleven cases, and the axis has zero coverage --- which is not an oversight to be quietly filled but a measurement: this initiative has been designing a representation for players and LLMs and has never once evaluated one from the position of the person writing the world. [C10](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c10-the-moonbase-computer-system) and [C11](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c11-the-rube-goldberg-machine) both *are* authored and both note that neither AB-14 trigger fires, so the corpus currently has improvisation at human scale and authorship above it, **with nothing crossing**. Phase 0 gains a requirement for it.

### What this frame does not do

**It does not rank the axes against each other**, and it must not be read as doing so. Positioning is listed first because it is the substrate, not because it wins ties. When apprehension and reasoning conflict on a case --- which the section above says they will, by construction --- **the frame names the conflict and does not resolve it.** Resolving it is a product call each time, and one that should be made visibly rather than derived from an ordering nobody agreed to.


**It also does not retire the AB rows.** Several of them are the axes' questions in older clothing --- AB-19's *description or mechanism* is **apprehension versus reasoning under an earlier name**, which is why the retro-fit was cheap. That convergence is evidence the axes are real, not licence to collapse the rows into them.

### Considered and declined: state as a sixth axis (2026-08-07)

**Recorded because "why isn't state an axis" is a question the next reader will ask too, and the answer is load-bearing rather than obvious.** Raised the day after **AB-1** resolved: should *state* join the frame as a sibling to positioning?

**It fails this section's own test on all three counts.** Positioning's audience is the world and its function is to filter; a state axis would have **the same audience** and **the same function** --- [C12](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c12-the-moonbase-power-system-and-the-cut-cable) fired exactly that filter, eliminating *no carrier anywhere*. And **no conflict was found**: C12's isomorphism runs six-for-six, so nothing good for recording arrangement is bad for recording causality. Per the paragraph above, *a benefit list with no conflicts in it is decoration.* **State has no audience the frame does not already have** --- the parties who care whether the door is powered are the player, the LLM and the author.

**It would also contradict AB-1.** Splitting the evaluation frame along the exact line the *representation* declined to split would have the frame assert a distinction the model denies. If the two needed separate requirement axes, they needed separate substrates.

**The instinct was catching something real, and it was not an axis.** [C12](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c12-the-moonbase-power-system-and-the-cut-cable) found that **coarsening is sound on containment and unsound on causality** --- which is not a requirement difference but **one mechanism behaving differently on two relation kinds**, and the frame had nowhere to record it. That gap is now [the relation-kind register](#the-relation-kind-register). **The lesson generalises: when the axes feel short of something, check whether it is a new axis or a missing per-kind fact, because the second is much more likely.**


---

## The relation-kind register

**Opened 2026-08-07, and it exists because **AB-1** created a debt the same day it resolved.** AB-1 came back as *one substrate, **distinct relation kinds** above it* --- and nothing anywhere lists what those kinds are or how they differ. **This is that list.**

**It is not a sixth axis** (see [Considered and declined](#considered-and-declined-state-as-a-sixth-axis-2026-08-07)). The axes are applied *through* it: a mechanism can satisfy the reasoning axis on one kind and violate it on another, which is exactly what [C12](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c12-the-moonbase-power-system-and-the-cut-cable) found and what the frame had nowhere to record.

**Status: seeded and unstable.** Three of the four kinds below are not built. **This does not graduate to [`AGENT.concepts.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.concepts.md) yet** --- a durable doc listing unbuilt edge kinds is close enough to the wishlist anti-pattern to matter, and unlike the fractal vocabulary this is nowhere near stable. **It graduates when the kinds stop moving**, which is a Phase 6 obligation and a real one: the register is the thing an implementer most needs and the thing least likely to be re-derived correctly.

| Kind | Directed? | Coarsening sound? | Built? | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **`PartOf`** --- root to part, inside a whole's own graph. *Registered as `part-of` until 2026-08-09; renamed when it became a concrete member of `HostRelationalEdgeKind`* | Yes, but as *containment* --- the asymmetry is structural, not causal | **Yes.** Coarsening generalises: "the bag's strap" -> "the bag" stays true | No --- **named as a kind 2026-08-09 (AB-48)**; the locked frame's clause 1 is stated over it | The kind every locked-frame claim is stated over. **Shard-defining:** a part travels with its whole, so this is an unconditional `carry` under transfer. **Intensional:** a crate implies a lid --- what a thing *is* implies its parts |
| **`In`** --- root to contents, inside the container's own graph | Yes, as containment; same structural asymmetry as `PartOf` | **Yes**, and transitively: crate in room, treasure in crate, so "the treasure is in the room" holds | No --- **new 2026-08-09 (AB-48)**, and the first relation of its family: the model previously could not say *in* about anything | **Shard-defining**, unconditional `carry` under transfer. **Extensional:** a crate does not imply treasure --- contents are enumerated, not implied. **Not exclusive with `PartOf`** --- a battery is *part of* a flashlight **and** *in* it ([C8](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c8-the-flashlight-that-vanished-when-someone-looked-at-it)), so the two may hold on one pair at once and neither is the other's negation |
| **`Held`** --- root to a thing in hand | Yes; structural asymmetry, as containment | **Yes.** `Theseus -Held-> thread:1` coarsens to *Theseus is holding the thread*, still true | No --- **new 2026-08-09**. Today "held" is membership in a character's graph, i.e. the ghost link again | **Shard-defining**, unconditional `carry`. **Distinguished from `In` by availability, which the fiction cares about:** held is usable now; `In` (a pack) is carried but not to hand. **Open: is it exclusive with `In` for one object** --- a sword is in hand or in the pack, not both --- and if so, exclusivity is a per-kind-pair fact rather than a property of membership |
| **`Worn`** --- root to a thing worn | Yes; same shape | **Yes**, same shape | No --- **new 2026-08-09** | **Shard-defining**, unconditional `carry`. **Distinguished from `Held` by capacity:** worn persists without occupying hands. **Open, and interesting: does `Worn` want a qualified endpoint on the *subject* side** --- a hat is worn *on the head* --- which would be the first use of endpoint addressing on the **from** end rather than the **to** end. **Not character-restricted:** a statue wears a crown, a coat rack holds coats, so `Held`/`Worn` do not make Character a privileged host kind |
| **Sibling / connection** --- part to part, expressing how they join | Not inherently | Presumed yes; **untested** | No --- AB-26; licensing labels are **PQ-8** | AB-26's correction stands: adjacency **licenses** an edge, never derives one. Shape must not be presumed a sequence |
| **Spatial** --- `On`, `Under`, `Against`, `Custom` | No | Yes | **Yes, shipped** --- same-host only, object-only endpoints | `applyStepSequenceCore` derives the shared host live and **throws** on mismatch |
| **`powers` / conducts** --- causal | **Yes, strictly.** A traversal that runs backwards produces nonsense | **No --- and this is the register's founding entry.** Coarsening a reachability fact *assumes reachability*: "the system is powered" -> "the door is powered" is **false** after a cut. Safe default is **`unknown`**, not the whole's answer | No --- no causal edge kind exists at all | Answers are **evaluated**, not looked up (**AB-39**). Pulls AB-6 toward *derived* where position pulls it toward *stored* |
| **`attached-to`** --- participation without parthood | Yes | Unexamined | No | The door is attached to the power system without being **part of** it. This is why AB-1 is *one substrate, distinct kinds* rather than one type |

**What each row must eventually say**, so later additions are comparable rather than ad hoc: directedness, whether coarsening is sound, whether the [summarization seam](#a-third-argument-on-a-third-axis-the-port-is-the-summarization-seam-2026-08-07) is an optimisation or a **correctness requirement**, what improvisation-minting means for it, and whether a port may join two edges of this kind to a different one. **Two more added 2026-08-07 from [P4](#proposal-p4-settling-dataflow-and-narrative-heat):** whether a propagating impulse **splits or replicates** at a branch (a pull divides between paths; a power cut broadcasts, and attenuating across a fan-out would leave a large system unable to notify its own edges), and whether **cycles are legal** on this kind --- composition is a DAG, causality legitimately is not. **The split-versus-replicate entry is the register's first *prediction* rather than a recorded observation**, which is what it was opened hoping to produce.

**A cross-kind question the per-row format cannot hold, opened 2026-08-10: do containment kinds compose *across* kinds?** `In` coarsens transitively **within** its own kind --- crate in room, treasure in crate, therefore treasure in room. **Whether a mixed chain composes has never been asked:** does *character `In` nook* plus *nook `PartOf` room* entail *character `In` room*? **It is not idle.** Character-in-room is the system's most load-bearing derived fact --- perception, movement, navigation all read it --- so if it can become a two-kind chain, that is a change to the hottest path in the model. **Two places it arrives, and only one is avoidable.** [AB-12(b)'s park](#open-decisions-design--plan-only) sidesteps it deliberately by requiring characters to take a **spatial** relation to a sub-space rather than a containment one, keeping `character In room` direct. **AB-30**'s wall work does not get that out: a wall is `PartOf` a room, and anything mounted to the wall is then related through a mixed chain. **Record the answer per kind-pair rather than as one rule**, on this register's founding finding --- soundness is a property of the kind, and there is no reason to expect it to be a property of *kinds* in the plural either.

**The register's first general finding, worth stating before it is buried in rows:** ***soundness of a mechanism is a property of the relation kind, not of the mechanism.*** Coarsening looked universal for two days because every kind it had met was containment-shaped. **Expect the same to be true of the other mechanisms** --- the seam, presence-by-absence, the coarsening defaults, single-use --- and check each against a causal kind before assuming it carries.

**The second general finding, added 2026-08-09 with `In` and `PartOf`: the kinds fall into families, and a kind's family decides *which graph its edge lives in*.** **Containment kinds (`In`, `PartOf`) are *shard-defining*:** the related node travels with the root, so its edge belongs in the root's own graph. **Spatial kinds (`On`, `Under`, `Against`, `Custom`) are not:** they relate siblings, and their edge belongs in the graph where both endpoints are siblings. **Worked case, and it is the one that produced the rule.** A crate with a crowbar **on** it, a lid **part of** it, and treasure **in** it: the lid and the treasure are nodes in the *crate's* graph; the crowbar is a node in the *room's* graph, with `crowbar On crate` a sibling edge there, exactly as today. **Why the placement is not a matter of taste:** shard membership silently overrides `classifyInteractionUnderTransfer`. Anything inside a shard travels unconditionally when the shard moves, so putting a spatial edge inside the container's graph would make lifting the crate carry the crowbar **regardless** of what the classifier returns for `On` --- the kind would become decorative and the classification would only ever get a vote on edges that *cross* a boundary. It would also re-create the eviction cost of [flatten-instead-of-nest](#considered-and-rejected-flatten-instead-of-nest), which P2 rejected: every pickup migrating nodes out of a shard they should never have been in.

**The third general finding, added 2026-08-09: relation *kind* and endpoint *granularity* are independent axes, and no question should fork between them.** A kind says **what the relationship is** (`In`, `PartOf`, `On`, `Held`, `TiedTo`); an endpoint address says **what it attaches to** --- the whole node, or a specific interior part of it via a port. **Any kind may take a qualified endpoint.** The proof is a spatial case: `bedpost -TiedTo-> thread:1` is not a containment relation, yet the qualification does exactly the same work it does for containment, so qualification cannot be a property of the containment family. **This retires a fork rather than adding a distinction** --- see AB-4, which carried *edges versus endpoint-addressing* as rival answers when they answer different questions. **The practical payoff is [held-and-present](#h3-ports-as-the-boundary-mechanism-decomposition-as-a-modellers-choice), which falls out with no new mechanism:** Theseus's graph holds `Theseus -Held-> thread:1`, the room's holds `room -In-> thread:2`, and the thread's own graph binds port 1 to its skein and port 2 to a span. **There was never a contradiction to resolve** --- there was one thread collapsed to a single referent, which is [C7](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c7-ariadnes-thread)'s own *where along* defect appearing as a false exclusivity.

**An untested prediction recorded rather than dropped, with its standing counter-case attached.** The intensional/extensional split above suggests **improvisation licence attaches to `PartOf` and not to `In`** --- crate-ness implies a lid, so a lid may be improvised; crate-ness implies no treasure. **Treat this as contested, not as a finding.** (i) It sits badly with **AB-45**, which resolved that *licence is carried by the whole*, not by an edge kind. (ii) **[C17](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c17-the-professional-kitchen-the-chefs-knife-and-the-stand-mixer-nobody-asked-about) already contests it** --- a chef's knife improvised into a kitchen is contents, not a part, and C17 is the corpus's authoring-primary case, built for licence. (iii) **Non-exclusivity may dissolve it entirely:** if a battery is both `PartOf` and `In` a flashlight, neither kind can discriminate licence, because both are true of one object at once. **Test against C8, C17, C18, C4 and C13, and seek negative cases deliberately** --- this is the register's second *prediction* rather than observation, and unlike the split-versus-replicate entry it arrives already contested.

### The naming consequence: `positionGraph` will be lying

**A downstream effect of AB-1 that no row owned, noted 2026-08-07 and deliberately not solved here.** If causal edges share the substrate, they live in **`EphemeraPositionGraph`**, in a lane called **`dataSource/positions`**, described by vocabulary that says a whole *"has its own `positionGraph`"* --- including the [graduated concepts entry](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.concepts.md#wholes-parts-and-ports). **None of those names will be true of what the structure holds.**

**Recorded now because it is cheap to record and expensive to discover mid-rename.** No code is in scope, so nothing is actionable today; the minimum is a **Phase 6 obligation**, and the question of whether it needs handling earlier --- the concepts entry is already written in the misleading vocabulary --- is **open and flagged for its own discussion**, which the naming question was judged to deserve on its own terms rather than as a footnote here.

---

## What already exists to build on

Read these before answering anything. Several are not background --- they are hooks the codebase has *already pre-registered* for this work, and re-deriving them from scratch would waste the design effort that put them there.

1. **The rooted-graph trigger, already written down.** [`positionGraph/AGENT.md` --- Known limitation (deferred)](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/positionGraph/AGENT.md#known-limitation-deferred) records that `EphemeraPositionGraph` is host-bound and unrooted, that [`CarryClosureFragment`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/positionGraph/expandValidate/interactionUnderTransfer.ts) --- `{ rootId, members, edges }` --- is "a rooted position graph in all but name," and that **if a root concept ever arrives, the fragment should collapse into it rather than persist as parallel duplication.** A composition abstraction is a named, persistent carry closure. That note is pinned in two places specifically so whoever adds a root here finds it. This is the single strongest existing anchor for axis 1.
   - One constraint travels with it: **`rootId` is recorded, never derived.** `computeCarryClosure` takes it from its `startId` argument because the BFS guards with `closureSet.has(...)`, so the traversal *tree* is order-dependent while the induced edge set is not. Any root concept must be an input.
2. **Object-as-host, already deferred rather than rejected.** [`AGENT.concepts.md` --- Fractal position graphs](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.concepts.md#fractal-position-graphs-container-scale-and-edges) states container scale shipped for Character hosts (D16) with "**Object `OBJECT#` / Area hosts deferred until needed**." Nesting is precisely the case that makes them needed. `EphemeraMembershipHostId` is Room | Character today.
3. **Improvisation as the minting precedent.** The objects lane already brings things into existence on demand, with a two-step existence-then-placement sequence, a compensating delete, and a diagnostics orphan sweep: [`objects/AGENT.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/objects/AGENT.md), and the contract's [Object room membership](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.contract.md#object-room-membership-nodes-only) section. Axis 1's minting question (Phase 3) should start from this shape, not invent one.
4. **Graph roles: same shape, different authority.** [`AGENT.concepts.md` --- Graph roles](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.concepts.md#graph-roles-shared-shape-different-authority) separates authored blueprint / play manipulation / materialized presentation / ephemeral presentation for the *same* `{ nodes, edges }` shape. That table exists because conflating them cost something before. An `Abstraction` will face the same fourfold question and should be answered with the same discipline.
5. **Trim-to-valid as the decay precedent.** The eviction ladder ([`AGENT.concepts.md` --- Eviction ladder](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.concepts.md#eviction-ladder)) answers "what survives when the world changes underneath a stored structure" by filtering to what is still valid, reading the top survivor, and **calling it a change only when the resolved answer differs**. AB-6 is structurally the same question.
6. **Projections are filters, not the graph.** Same concepts doc, core vocabulary. AB-9 leans hard on this: if level-selection is a projection concern, a large fraction of the LLM-scalability payoff lands in the presentation lane and never becomes stored truth.
