**A graph is static structure; a network is a medium things propagate through.** If causality works by impulses settling and heat flowing back the other way, ***Ludic Network* stops being a nicer word for the ensemble and starts describing what the ensemble does** --- a second, independent justification for the term recorded under [the naming consequence](#the-naming-consequence-positiongraph-will-be-lying), and a stronger one than kind-neutrality alone.

---

## Proposal P5: improvisational licence as a first-class graph item

**Recorded 2026-08-09 from conversation. Not promoted to a working hypothesis** --- H3 is in flight, and [candidate H2](#candidate-h2-not-adopted-ab-12s-privileged-level-reframe)'s reason for one-at-a-time still holds.

**Status, restated 2026-08-09 after [PL-1's iteration 1](#pl-1-iteration-1-brute-force-relevance-classified-as-a-prototype-2026-08-09) was elected.** P5 splits in two for status purposes, and conflating the halves is what made its promotion question look harder than it is. **The shape half** --- [clauses](#the-scheme-as-a-clause-table-numbered-2026-08-09) 1, 2, 3 and 7 --- **is LOCKED as of 2026-08-09**, after C20 attacked P5 clause 1 and vindicated it while falsifying P5 clause 2 into its repaired content/manner form. See [Locked: P5's shape clauses](#locked-p5s-shape-clauses-2026-08-09) for what is **not** locked and must not ride in on it, and for the proposal-level rollback trigger. **The utility half depends on relevance**, and relevance is now **buildable rather than unsolved**: a candidate is elected, an iteration 2 is named, and rollback triggers are set. **So P5 is blocked on a build, not on an open problem** --- which is the condition under which locking its shape clauses ahead of its utility is honest rather than premature.

**Written as a section rather than a row on this plan's own stated criterion** --- [P2](#proposal-p2-separate-the-storage-shard-from-the-containment-relation) is a section "because it touches AB-2, AB-3, AB-4, AB-9, AB-16, AB-30 and AB-32." P5 touches **AB-9, AB-14, AB-19, AB-29, AB-42, AB-43, AB-44 and AB-45**, reorganizes all four of the negatives-cluster rows, and reaches [C17](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c17-the-professional-kitchen-the-chefs-knife-and-the-stand-mixer-nobody-asked-about) and [C18](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c18-three-place-settings-and-only-two-of-them-may-have-a-napkin). Those rows stay open and are **restated in licence terms** rather than absorbed.

### The scheme, as a clause table (numbered 2026-08-09)

**Improvisational licence is a first-class item in the graph, attached to an entity, and manipulable like anything else in it.**

**Numbered on [H3](#h3-ports-as-the-boundary-mechanism-decomposition-as-a-modellers-choice)'s precedent, and *before* the first falsification case rather than after** --- H3's nine clauses exist so a failure lands somewhere specific and rolls back per clause, and P5 was three clauses of prose carrying a great many consequences. **The exercise paid for itself immediately: numbering surfaced four claims P5 was already relying on without stating** --- P5 clauses 9, 10 and 11, plus a refinement to 7 that its source case had conflated. **An unstated claim cannot be falsified, so those four were unfalsifiable until now**, which is the argument for doing this first.

| # | Clause | Status |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **1** | **Licence is unbounded by default.** An entity with no recorded limit permits any improvisation within its scope | **Established.** [C17](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c17-the-professional-kitchen-the-chefs-knife-and-the-stand-mixer-nobody-asked-about)'s three-value model stated from the other side --- *not established* costs nothing, which is what lets an infinite detail space go unrepresented. **Vindicated 2026-08-09 by [C20](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c20-the-locked-room-and-nothing-obvious), which was built to attack it and found it did not need attacking:** a locked-room mystery needs no closed world, only a high-weight **manner** narrowing (P5 clause 2), so unbounded-by-default survives with the three-value model intact. **Pressure examined and found narrower than first claimed, 2026-08-09.** [Coyote's `environmentAffordances`](#prior-art-in-shipped-code-coyotes-two-affordance-branches-2026-08-09) is a **closed reasoning vocabulary** whose absences are decidable --- but it constrains what a reasoning step is *shown*, not what the world *contains*, and reading it as closed-world was an over-claim withdrawn the same day. **The clause is not falsified.** What it gains is a sharper question for C20: *does a closed reasoning vocabulary license a closed world?* **This is now the clause C20 attacks** --- re-aimed here on 2026-08-09 from P5 clause 3, which turned out to be a wording problem rather than a contradiction. The tightly-authored closed world, where an author wants silence to mean **no**, is the falsification target, and it is **independent of relevance**, which is why it replaced the tavern version |
| **2** | **A negative is a recorded *narrowing* of licence, not a fact asserted about the world.** *There is no deep fryer* is *the kitchen's licence, closed over deep-fryer-shaped things*; a blanket negative is a narrowing to zero. **Narrowings are of two kinds: *content* (a set of things that may not be minted) and *manner* (a way of minting that is not permitted)** | **Established, and falsified 2026-08-09 in an assumption it never stated.** [C20](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c20-the-locked-room-and-nothing-obvious): every worked instance had been **extensional** --- *no deep fryer*, *no napkins*, a place setting closed to further members --- while *no **obvious** entrances* denotes a **predicate that must be evaluated**, undecidable by lookup at any vocabulary size. **The manner/content split above is the repair.** **Two consequences:** a **second judgment point** beside PL-1's (whether a candidate falls *inside* a narrowing, not only which narrowings are relevant), and **AB-43 gets materially worse** --- some regions have no descriptive answer, only a predicate. **Found by numbering the clauses**, which is what that exercise was for: an unstated assumption cannot be falsified. | The inversion [C18](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c18-three-place-settings-and-only-two-of-them-may-have-a-napkin) Finding 5 recorded, and what dissolved AB-43's blanket half. Also what keeps C17's closed-world defect from returning: a narrowing is not a completeness claim |
| **3** | **Scope is the entity the licence attaches to, and an entity's scope includes what it contains** --- so a limit reaches **inward** freely and never **outward** | **Established, after a correction on 2026-08-09.** Three independent arrivals support the no-outward half (C18's hungry absence, C19's mis-scoped genre rule, the fault section). ~~**Under direct attack in the other direction** --- a tavern authored *poor* must reach inward, and this clause forbids it; the clause is required to both not-reach and reach, C11's internal-contradiction mode.~~ **Withdrawn: the contradiction was an artifact of the original wording**, which said *no further* without saying **in which direction**. A place setting's limit reaching the tavern is **outside** its holder-scope and forbidden; the tavern's limit reaching a place setting **inside** it is **within** its holder-scope and permitted. **Containment scope is directional by nature**, and reading *no further* as direction-neutral was the error. **Note what the clause does and does not do now:** it says a container's limits *may* reach inward; **which** of them do is [PL-1](#open-questions-p5-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused)'s question, not this clause's. The clause permits; relevance selects |
| **4** | **Licence is a first-class graph item, attached to an entity and manipulable** | **Open --- this is AB-42.** C19 shows a licence that *changes*, which supports *manipulable* but says nothing about node-versus-collection |
| **5** | **Licence import is a whole acquiring an intensional description** --- no mechanism of its own | **Established, on one case.** [C19](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c19-filling-out-the-place-setting-and-the-tavern-that-ran-out-of-napkins) beat 3. Notable for falling out of C18's existing discriminator rather than needing new machinery, which is this plan's weak evidence-of-a-real-joint signal --- but one case is one case |
| **6** | **Import is a copy, not a reference; licence provenance is not traced and licence changes do not cascade** | **Settled as *policy*, with a re-open trigger --- a different category from the shape clauses and it must not be swept in with them.** See [Licence transfer](#licence-transfer-import-is-a-copy-and-provenance-is-deliberately-not-traced-2026-08-09). Defended on cost *and* on the local answer being the fictionally correct one |
| **7** | **Whether a whole has an *intensional* description or is merely *extensional* determines whether it has a licence at all** | **Established by C18 --- and corrected here.** C18 states the discriminator as *intensional -> open, extensional -> closed*. **That conflates two things.** A *poor* tavern is intensional and **narrow**, so intensionality determines whether a licence **exists and what shape it has**, not whether it is open. **Found by numbering, before C20 was written** |
| **8** | **Causality propagates; licence does not.** Permission is held, not transmitted --- register propagation policy *none* | **Established on one case (C19), and a deliberate asymmetry with [P4](#proposal-p4-settling-dataflow-and-narrative-heat) rather than an oversight.** Note P5 clause 3's attack does not touch this: *reaching inward by scope* is not the same as *propagating along edges* |
| **9** | **A licence is a *shaped space*, not a scalar** --- an entity may be open in some dimensions and closed in others | **Open, and unstated in P5 until now.** Surfaced by C20's *warm but poor* tavern: warmth and poverty are simultaneous and pull opposite ways, so a single openness dial cannot express it. **Puts real weight on AB-43's unsolved half** (describing a closed region) and on AB-42 (what a licence record is) |
| **10** | ~~**Entities not related by import have no licence relationship.**~~ **Restated 2026-08-09: permission is *copied* at import; context is *read live* via relevance** | ~~Open, the gap C20 targets second.~~ **Substantially resolved rather than falsified, and the resolution is the useful kind --- it names a distinction that was missing rather than removing one.** The original claim was a consequence of P5 clause 3 read direction-neutrally; once P5 clause 3 permits inward reach, a never-imported entity **does** have a relationship to its container --- it reads ancestor facts live, selected by [PL-1](#open-questions-p5-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused). **So *imported-from* and *merely-inside* differ as snapshot versus live read**, which also stops P5 clause 6's no-cascade from conflicting with live relevance: **the thing that does not cascade is *permission*; the thing read live is *context*.** Residual work belongs to PL-1, not here |
| **11** | **Authoring sets licence unattenuated; improvisation operates only within licence** | **Open --- proposed in discussion, never worked.** Authoring is not delegation, so it is outside any attenuation scheme; this is also the third route by which the asset-added branch of AB-29 arrives. C20 has an *authorially* constrained tavern, so it exercises this clause directly |

**Tagging convention, on H3's terms:** mark a row `P5-dependent` only when it is **derived from** a clause, not merely compatible with one --- and **cite the clause number**, since the whole point of numbering is that P5 clause 6 falling should not revert rows that only ever needed P5 clause 2.

### What the reframe buys, stated as four specific repairs

| Problem, as previously recorded | Under P5 |
| --- | --- |
| **The completeness trap** ([C18](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c18-three-place-settings-and-only-two-of-them-may-have-a-napkin) Finding 4) --- *no napkin here* must not be read as *no napkins in the tavern* | **Becomes unrepresentable rather than forbidden.** The limit attaches to the place setting, so it cannot reach the tavern. **A rule became an invariant**, which is the stronger form and the reason to prefer this framing over the warning it replaces |
| **AB-43** --- what is a negative keyed by, when its referent does not exist? | **Dissolved for the blanket case** (keyed by the licensing entity, an id that exists) and **reshaped for the narrowing case**: no longer *give identity to a nonentity*, which is ill-posed, but *describe a closed region of licence space*, which is well-posed and comparably hard |
| **AB-42's accumulation cost** --- negatives are cheaper to create than positives and accrue faster | **Collapses from N nodes to one licence per entity whose content narrows.** And a licence **coarsens** (*this place setting is closed*) where independent negative nodes do not --- which matters precisely because the cost was about LLM context |
| **AB-45's provenance collision** --- licence is provenance-determined, but AB-14 holds provenance is invisible downstream | **Resolved.** Licence is carried *by the whole* as a first-class property; provenance only sets its initial value. Nothing downstream introspects how the whole came to exist, so the merge-layer claim survives intact |

**One consistency check, run deliberately because a unification that quietly costs a settled property is the failure mode:** *not established* still costs nothing. Licence is recorded only when **narrowed**, so C17's three-value model is preserved rather than traded away.

### What it does not resolve

- **AB-43 for narrowing licences** --- describing a closed region is still unanswered.
- **Whether licence-closure and established-absence are the same fact.** *There is no battery* must be a **world** fact for the deduction *therefore it is dark* to run; *this place setting is closed* is only a permission. They plausibly coincide, since the only route to a battery appearing there is improvisation --- **except through authoring**, which is not delegated and can add a deep fryer to a kitchen whose licence is closed. **That exception is [AB-29](#open-decisions-design--plan-only)'s asset-added branch arriving from a third direction.** AB-44 therefore survives in narrower form: **every established negative closes licence; only some also carry causal force.**
- **Composition of licences** --- how nested and layered licences combine, and how positives and negatives merge under manipulation. **Open; the leading frame is capability attenuation** (a delegated authority may only narrow), with **authoring explicitly outside it**. **Subsumed as the licence-shaped half of [PL-1](#open-questions-p5-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused)**, which asks the same question over positive facts as well.
- **Divergence** --- see the cost below.

### Licence transfer: import is a copy, and provenance is deliberately not traced (2026-08-09)

**The operation, from [C19](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c19-filling-out-the-place-setting-and-the-tavern-that-ran-out-of-napkins).** A recognized place setting is licence-zero. The player says *"fill out the place setting with everything else it needs."* Licence is **imported** from the tavern --- which has an open licence to improvise tavern furnishings --- into the place setting, whose limit is removed. **It thereafter holds its own, independent licence.**

**What the import actually is, and it is not a new mechanism.** [C18](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c18-three-place-settings-and-only-two-of-them-may-have-a-napkin)'s discriminator is **intensional versus extensional**: a recognized whole is extensional --- its member set *is* its content --- which is exactly why its licence is zero. The player's utterance supplies an **intensional description** (*everything a place setting needs*). **Licence import is a whole acquiring an intensional description.** **So C18's three cases are three *states*, not three kinds, and there is an operation between them** --- which C18 did not say and, read alone, implies the opposite. **The negative lock is not permanent**, and nothing in the model made it so.

**Import is a copy, not a reference.** Once imported, the licence is the place setting's own. A later tavern-level narrowing --- *the tavern has no napkins in stock* --- **does not reach into it**, and the place setting may still improvise a napkin.

**Explicitly declined, and recorded as a decision rather than an omission: licence provenance is not traced and licence changes do not cascade.** It is *possible* to keep the provenance graph of every imported licence and propagate narrowings through it. **It is not worth it:** an immense standing cost --- every licence read potentially walking a history, every narrowing potentially cascading --- to correct a small set of edge cases. **This plan is choosing not to be constrained by it.**

**And the local reading is usually the *fictionally correct* one, which upgrades this from a compromise to a preference.** The napkin was laid out before anyone found the storeroom empty; a tavern being out of napkins **now** is a claim about stock, not about what is already set on a table. **The cheaper answer is also the better one in the motivating case**, which is worth stating so this is not remembered as a scalability concession.

**The one case where it does bite, and the repair is scope rather than propagation.** If *no napkins* is meant as a **world or genre rule** rather than a stock fact, a place setting improvising one is genuinely wrong. **The fix is the move that killed the scope trap, arriving a third time:** attach the limit at the scope it actually holds at. A genre rule is not the tavern's licence and should never have been recorded as one. **Propagation is the wrong repair for a mis-scoped limit.**

**The principle this establishes, stated because it is a deliberate asymmetry with [P4](#proposal-p4-settling-dataflow-and-narrative-heat):** **causality propagates; licence does not.** Permission has no conservation law and no downstream obligation --- a licence is held, not transmitted. As a [relation-kind register](#the-relation-kind-register) entry, licence's propagation policy is **none**, which no other kind in the register has.

**Re-open trigger, set now while it costs nothing --- and set at *consequence*, not at visibility.** ~~A player-visible contradiction.~~ **Raised 2026-08-09, the same day, and the correction matters:** player-visible is the **minimum** bar, not the bar. Per [Fiction is a fault-tolerant medium](#fiction-is-a-fault-tolerant-medium--and-what-that-does-not-excuse-2026-08-09), a contradiction a player merely *notices* is inside the tolerance --- audiences do repair work, and the alternatives to accepting that are all worse. **The trigger is a contradiction a player *acted on*** --- reasoning from an absence and being contradicted by the world is agency undermined --- **or one that breaks a stake the fiction had established.** Also unconditionally: **any *structural* defect** (two napkins where there is one, a mis-keyed referent) is a bug regardless, never covered by this decision, and never a reason to re-open it either. **And weight the trigger by salience** --- a contradiction the fiction never leaned on is cheap even when noticed; one that contradicts something a scene was *about* is not.

### Locked: P5's shape clauses (2026-08-09)

**On [the parts-and-ports precedent](#locked-frame-parts-and-ports-2026-08-06), and with the same discipline: lock the shape, enumerate loudly what must not ride in on it.**

**Locked --- P5 clauses 1, 2, 3 and 7.** Unbounded by default; a negative is a recorded **narrowing** (of two kinds, **content** and **manner**); scope is the entity **plus what it contains**, so limits reach inward and never outward; and **intensional versus extensional** decides whether a whole has a licence and what shape it takes. **Each is corpus-grounded rather than argued** --- P5 clause 1 was attacked by [C20](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c20-the-locked-room-and-nothing-obvious) and vindicated, P5 clause 2 was falsified by C20 and **locks in its repaired form**, P5 clause 3 has three independent arrivals, P5 clause 7 comes from [C18](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c18-three-place-settings-and-only-two-of-them-may-have-a-napkin) and was corrected by the numbering pass.

**Not locked, and must not ride in on this.** **Representation** (P5 clause 4 / AB-42 --- node or collection, undecided). **Composition** of nested licences (PL-1's licence-shaped half, unworked; capability attenuation still an unexercised candidate). **Narrative weight** (AB-46, deliberately undesigned). **Transfer-is-copy** (P5 clause 6) --- settled as *policy with a re-open trigger*, **a different category from a shape claim**, and it must not acquire lock status by proximity. **Licence as the generative half of setting character** (speculative). **Genre boundaries** (AB-47) --- **a different construct entirely**; locking P5 locks nothing about them.

**Consequences accepted by locking, recorded so they are owned rather than rediscovered.** **(i)** Manner narrowings require **judgment to apply**, so AB-43 has no purely descriptive answer for some regions --- locked with that cost attached rather than pending its resolution. **(ii)** P5's **utility** remains gated on [PL-1 iteration 1](#pl-1-iteration-1-brute-force-relevance-classified-as-a-prototype-2026-08-09); only its **shape** is locked. **(iii)** **Locking ahead of a killing test is deliberate.** No case has yet tried to destroy P5 --- C20's worst outcome was a repair --- and the mitigation is the trigger below rather than a claim that none exists.

**What would re-open a locked clause --- the proposal-level rollback trigger, set now.** **If licence records turn out to be reconstructible from ordinary established facts, with no behaviour distinguishing them, then licence is not a construct and P5 failed** --- negatives-as-narrowings would collapse back into plain facts wearing a new name. **Measurable from iteration 1's data, and it fires against the proposal rather than the implementation**, which is the property [P4's own trigger](#what-p4-being-load-bearing-here-means-2026-08-07) was built to have. **Second re-opener:** a case exhibiting a **true internal contradiction** --- one restriction that must be simultaneously in force and not in force for a single entity, [C11](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c11-the-rube-goldberg-machine)'s mode. **Named so the record shows this was considered and not merely unimagined; no candidate scenario exists.**

#### Why the paradox re-opener is less threatening here than in a simulationist model (2026-08-09)

**The concern is real for the wrong kind of system.** In a deductive knowledge representation a contradiction is catastrophic because of **explosion** --- from a contradiction, anything follows, so one bad fact corrupts every inference downstream of it. **That is the reason logical KR treats paradox as fatal**, and it is worth naming, because inheriting the fear without inheriting the mechanism would be a mistake.

**This system does no deductive closure.** It assembles a bounded packet and hands it to a reasoning step. **A contradiction therefore does not propagate --- it sits there being odd**, and the blast radius is bounded by the medium rather than by the logic. **Under [the feedback-regime frame](#the-feedback-regime-is-the-thing-to-measure-and-it-decides-which-solutions-to-look-for-2026-08-09) a contradiction is maximally *amplifying* in a deductive system and *damping* in a narration system** --- nobody builds on it, and narration papers it over. **Third time that frame has predicted a result before the argument for it was made.**

**And the medium is on our side in a way a simulation's is not: fiction is *supposed* to make sense.** Simulated reality routinely produces edge cases nobody intended, because it is not authored toward coherence; **fiction is produced by minds that want it to hang together, and audiences actively repair what does not.** Where fiction does contain paradox it is nearly always **owned as content** --- time travel, unreliable narration --- rather than suffered as a system failure.

**The adversarial case is handled by machinery already adopted, not by consistency maintenance.** A player *constructing* a paradox on purpose is attempting a mint; the [gate refuses and the system steadily refuses to reason about it](#the-cost-that-is-not-optional-and-it-is-ab-19s). **We never have to resolve the paradox --- only decline to build on it** --- and the refusal is already a moderation signal per [C21 Finding 7](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#finding-7-the-gate-produces-moderation-instrumentation-for-free-and-that-may-be-the-larger-prize).

#### How fiction actually resolves a contradiction: it registers a mystery (2026-08-09)

**The stronger form of the argument above, and it names a mechanism rather than a disposition.** *Elf King Ozymand died twenty thousand years ago* and *Elf King Ozymand fought the great drake Grimhond two hundred years ago* are flatly contradictory. **A play-to-find-out GM does not reconcile them and does not retract either.** They say *"Right? Sounds impossible. I wonder what's going on there,"* and move on --- **the contradiction registered as a mystery, to be solved through play or ignored.**

**Three things follow, and the first is the one that matters most.**

1. **No retraction is required, and that is the fourth arrival of this plan's *not a knowledge base* theme.** A logic KR must retract one fact or quarantine both. **Fiction keeps both**, because the graph holds **established fiction**, not asserted truths --- the same move [P4's dataflow reframe](#retraction-dissolves-under-a-dataflow-reading-and-that-reading-is-the-ludic-native-one) made when it found *the graph holds states, not beliefs* and retraction dissolved. **Two independent routes to *do not retract*, from causality and from consistency.**
2. **This is [C20](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c20-the-locked-room-and-nothing-obvious)'s mystery construct, generalised --- and C20 found it first for a different input.** *Nothing obvious* converts an **unknown** into a mystery; Ozymand converts a **contradiction** into one. **Same downstream object** --- a flagged question carrying a promise that an answer exists and is worth earning --- **from opposite inputs** (too little established, versus too much and conflicting). **So *mystery* is not a puzzle-specific device; it is the system's general defer-with-interest primitive**, and it needs no new state: a mystery is a **decorated not-established**, inheriting [C17](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c17-the-professional-kitchen-the-chefs-knife-and-the-stand-mixer-nobody-asked-about)'s *settled, not pending* property.
3. **"Or ignored" is load-bearing and easy to skim past.** A registered mystery carries **no obligation** to be resolved. That is what makes it cost one sentence, and it is why this does not smuggle in the unbounded-worklist problem [PS-10](#open-questions-p4-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused) is still wrestling with on the impulse side.

**Prior art, named on the same footing as reincorporation.** *Play to find out what happens* is Apocalypse World's agenda, and this is the **second load-bearing frame this plan has taken from theatre and tabletop craft** rather than from computing --- after [Johnstone's reincorporation](#narrative-integrity-is-in-service-to-reincorporation-2026-08-09). Worth noticing as a pattern in where the useful answers have been coming from.

**The precondition the mechanism has, and it is not optional: mystery is cheap only where nobody is vested.** Registering *I wonder what's going on there* defers reconciliation --- **and deferral is free exactly when no one is waiting on the answer.** If a player were invested in Ozymand having been continuously dead for twenty thousand years, contradicting it risks precisely the injury [narrative integrity](#narrative-integrity-is-in-service-to-reincorporation-2026-08-09) exists to prevent: their contribution not being valued or built on. **Deferring then is not neutral --- it leaves them waiting to learn whether their contribution survived**, which is an unresolved social tension rather than an intriguing loose end.

**That is why GM fiction contradicting *itself* is so much easier than players contradicting *each other*, and the provenance split already predicts the whole gradient:**

| Contradiction between | Cost |
| --- | --- |
| **system-improvised ↔ system-improvised** | **Trivial.** Mystery, or simply pick one. Nobody is vested |
| **system-improvised ↔ authored or player-driven** | **Asymmetric and easy.** The weighted side stands; the improvised side yields |
| **authored ↔ player-driven** | Hard --- and it is [AB-29](#open-decisions-design--plan-only)'s asset-added branch, already open |
| **player-driven ↔ player-driven** | **Hardest, and the mystery move does not defuse it** --- both contributors are waiting, so deferral costs rather than saves |

**Two consequences worth carrying.** **(i) [AB-46](#open-decisions-design--plan-only) gains a third job: weight decides whether a contradiction may be *deferred*.** After *how much contradicting it strains* and *the effort required to overturn it*, that is **three independent uses landing on one scalar**, which is evidence the quantity is real rather than an annotation invented per problem. **(ii) The favourable half, and it bears directly on [the feedback regime](#the-feedback-regime-is-the-thing-to-measure-and-it-decides-which-solutions-to-look-for-2026-08-09):** in this system the GM **is** the LLM, so **machine-generated fiction is system-improvised and therefore the *lowest-weight* kind by construction.** The contradictions an LLM in the loop produces are overwhelmingly the absorbable ones; **the expensive contradictions are player-against-player, which the LLM is not the author of.** That is a real narrowing of the fault-propagation worry rather than a reassurance about it.

**And where deferral is not affordable, the repair is not to pick a winner.** The craft move is to find the reading in which **both** contributions stand --- which is what the Ozymand mystery does when nobody is vested, and what must actually be *performed* rather than promised when someone is. The out-of-character channel [AB-47](#open-decisions-design--plan-only) identifies for table negotiation is the fallback when even that fails.

##### Correction: a mystery caps a *minimum effort*, not a maximum time (2026-08-09)

~~***Mystery* is the degenerate, zero-stakes case of reconciliation, not a separate mechanism.**~~ **Withdrawn the same day, and it had the construct backwards --- it described a floor as a ceiling.** That framing reads a mystery as **deferral**: reconciliation postponed, resolution arriving eventually. **The actual point of a first-class mystery object is the opposite: it caps a *minimum effort*** --- *this is unknown and **will not be revealed** before a certain amount of player investment.* **The floor is the whole construct.** A mystery whose answer is available on request is worthless; the locked room would be solved by asking.

**Three things this fixes, and the first dissolves a separate construct rather than adding one.**

1. **A mystery *is* a [manner narrowing](#the-scheme-as-a-clause-table-numbered-2026-08-09), applied to something not yet established.** [C20](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c20-the-locked-room-and-nothing-obvious) split narrowings into **content** (a set that may not be minted) and **manner** (a way of minting that is not permitted). **A mystery is exactly a manner narrowing on an unknown: the answer may be established, but only by an earned manner.** So this needs no new construct --- it is P5 clause 2's repair, reaching the case that motivated it.
2. **It *creates* stakes rather than lacking them, which is what the withdrawn phrasing got wrong.** Registering a mystery says *this is worth something and it will cost you*. Calling that zero-stakes inverted the claim.
3. **"Or ignored" stops needing separate justification.** Under a deferral reading, an unresolved mystery is a dangling loose end requiring an argument for why that is acceptable. **Under a floor reading it is simply *nobody paid the price*** --- a complete and correct outcome, not an omission.

**And it makes the construct mechanically real, which *first-class mystery object* was already asserting.** A minimum-investment floor is a **predicate a gate can evaluate** --- has the work been done? --- where *deferred reconciliation* is not checkable at all.

**One extension to [AB-46](#open-decisions-design--plan-only) follows, and it is symmetric enough to be worth having.** That row defines weight as **effort required to overturn**, which applies to things already established. **A mystery has no content and still has a floor** --- so the same scalar runs the other way as **effort required to *establish***. **One quantity, measured forward for the not-yet-established and backward for the established**, covering the whole lifecycle rather than half of it.

**The limit, stated so this does not get over-applied --- and it is the well-formedness line again.** **Contradictory *fiction* absorbs; contradictory *machinery* does not.** A mint gate must return yes or no, so a licence that both permits and forbids is not a mystery to be registered --- it is a code path that cannot decide, and it needs a **tie-break rule** (fail-safe: refuse). **That reduces the second re-opener further without dissolving it:** the machinery needs a defensible tie-break, and a case where no sensible tie-break exists would still be a real problem.

**What none of this excuses, on the standing discipline:** a paradox that produces a **structural** defect --- two place settings where there was one --- is still a bug. **Coherence is negotiable; well-formedness is not**, and that line does not move because the contradiction was interesting.

### Prior art in shipped code: Coyote's two affordance branches (2026-08-09)

**Checked against code, not recalled.** [`coyotePlanAffinities.ts`](../../../../../packages/mtw-interfaces/ts/coyotePlanAffinities.ts) gives `CoyoteTropeAffinity` two optional affordance fields, and **they are not two flavours of one thing --- they are the container side and the contributor side, and they differ on every axis that matters here.**

| | `environmentAffordances` | `affordancesProvided` |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Shape** | `{ object: EnvironmentAffordanceObject; roles }` | `{ object: string; intended?: true; roles }` |
| **Vocabulary** | **Closed enum** --- `boulder \| cactus \| tumbleweed \| rock-wall \| long-fall` | **Open string** |
| **Who supplies it** | The **location** | The **item** |
| **Container filtering** | [`filterTropeAffinitiesByRoom.ts`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/objects/filterTropeAffinitiesByRoom.ts) **removes** entries not available in the room | **None**, and the module says so explicitly: *"Room rules filter only `environmentAffordances`"* |
| **Direction** | **Constrains** --- the room narrows what a staged object may claim | **Contributes** --- the item adds to what is available |

**Five things this maps onto --- and one seam to declare first, because it bounds all of them.** **Coyote is a *reasoning-axis* system and P5 is an *authoring-axis* proposal.** `filterTropeAffinitiesByRoom` governs what a reasoning step is **shown**; P5's licence governs what may be **minted**. The mapping below is therefore strongest for [PL-1](#open-questions-p5-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused) --- which *is* the reasoning-axis question inside P5 --- and weakest for the licence clauses. **Recorded because the first draft of this section did not flag the seam and over-claimed on the strength of it.**

1. **[P5 clause 3](#the-scheme-as-a-clause-table-numbered-2026-08-09)'s inward reach has a shipped *analogue*.** `filterTropeAffinitiesByRoom(roomId)(tropeAffinities)` takes a **room** and narrows what an **object staged in it** may claim --- a container reaching a contained entity. **Downgraded from *instance* to *analogue* 2026-08-09, same day, on the correction below:** what the room narrows is the object's **reasoning inputs**, not its licence, so this confirms the *direction* is natural and workable rather than confirming the clause itself. **Still worth having** --- the shape this plan spent a day mistakenly calling a contradiction is running in production --- but it is not the clause.
2. **[PL-1](#open-questions-p5-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused) is shipped in hardcoded form.** That filter *is* relevance selection: of the environment facts a container holds, which reach the contained thing's reasoning? Its answer is a `switch` over three hardcoded room sets (`ROOM_IDS_WITH_ROCK_WALL`, `ROOM_IDS_WITH_LONG_FALL`, `ROOM_IDS_WITHOUT_CACTUS`). **Seventh time this initiative has found infrastructure pre-registered for a need it had not yet articulated** --- and the first time the pre-registration is of the *central open question* rather than a mechanism.
3. **Enforcement happens at *context-assembly* time --- a third enforcement point, and probably the best one.** [AB-47](#open-decisions-design--plan-only) settled on a **mint-time** gate and rejected a **narration-time** one. Coyote does neither: it filters what the reasoning step is *shown*. **If `rock-wall` is not in the packet, no plan proposes one and there is nothing to refuse.** That is the *refusal is structurally silent* insight, already implemented --- prevention by **omission** rather than by rejection, and it does not need a refusal message because nothing was attempted.
4. **`aptness` is a shipped relevance threshold.** `COYOTE_AFFINITY_APTNESS_MIN = 0.2`, with entries below it dropped. Graded relevance with a cutoff, which is PL-1's shape again.
5. **`intended?: true` is a candidate weight marker.** It distinguishes affordances an object was *meant* to provide from incidental ones --- plausibly a two-valued **AB-46**. **Flagged, not asserted**: its consumers were not traced, and a boolean is a thin basis for a claim about a graded concept.

#### A closed *reasoning vocabulary*, not a closed *world* --- and the difference is the whole question (corrected 2026-08-09)

~~**The falsification: P5 clause 1 is wrong for closed vocabularies, and shipped code says so.** Environment affordances are closed-world: a room absent from `ROOM_IDS_WITH_ROCK_WALL` has no rock wall, silence means **no**, and that is exactly what P5 clause 1 denies.~~ **Over-claimed, and withdrawn the same day it was written.** `filterTropeAffinitiesByRoom` says *do not reason with a rock wall here*; it does **not** say *there is no rock wall here*. **Those coincide in Coyote only because nothing else ever surfaces environment objects** --- which is exactly the *circumstantial, not structural* criticism [Coyote as prior art](#coyote-as-prior-art-bounded-llm-reasoning-that-is-not-bounded-structurally) already makes, arriving here against a claim this plan had just made itself.

**What survives is narrower and still useful: a closed *reasoning* vocabulary is legitimate, shipped, and correct** --- because the five entries are authored and exhaustive **for the purpose of bounding a reasoning packet.**

**The enum's selection criterion, checked rather than assumed, because it sharpens this:** the five are not desert nouns. `long-fall` is not an object anyone finds --- it is a property of a place. Boulder, cactus, tumbleweed, rock-wall and long-fall are the **standard Coyote gag affordances**; **lizards and sand are perfectly desert-justified and excluded anyway**, because neither affords a gag. **The vocabulary was drawn around what the reasoning step needs, not around what the setting contains** --- which is why reading it as a claim about the world was wrong.

**And the open side proves the point:** `affordancesProvided` is an open string, so `{ object: 'lizard', roles: ['Bait'] }` is representable today. **The closed enum constrains the *environment as a source*, not the fiction.** A thing may provide a lizard; the desert may not.

**The rule that survives, stated for the reasoning axis where it belongs:** *things a professional kitchen might have* is unbounded, so no packet can enumerate it and [C17](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c17-the-professional-kitchen-the-chefs-knife-and-the-stand-mixer-nobody-asked-about)'s three values are forced. *Environment affordances* is **five items**, authored and exhaustive, so a packet can carry the whole vocabulary and **absence from it is decidable**. **The three-value model collapses to two whenever the vocabulary is genuinely enumerable --- for the consumer that only sees the packet.** Whether it also collapses for the **world** is C20's question, not this section's.

**So [C20](#recommended-order) is *not* pre-answered, and P5 clause 1 is not falsified.** The question it owns is intact and now stated more precisely: **does a closed *reasoning* vocabulary license a closed *world*?** Coyote never faces it, because in a five-room demo the two are indistinguishable. **What C20 gains is a worked example of the legitimate half** --- closed vocabularies bounding a packet --- **and a sharpened axis to isolate: vocabulary-openness, and whether an author may close an open domain by fiat.**

**One general principle falls out of the vocabulary asymmetry, and it explains why [AB-43](#open-decisions-design--plan-only) is hard.** The **constraining** side is a closed enum; the **contributing** side is an open string. That is not incidental --- **a constraint needs a decidable membership test** (does this proposed thing fall in the closed region?) **and a contribution does not** (it just adds). **Minting is easy and narrowing is hard for that reason**, which is AB-43's difficulty restated as a property rather than a puzzle.

### Open questions (P5 sub-questions; ids stable, never reused)

**Parallel to P3's PQ series and P4's PS series.**

| ID | Question |
| --- | --- |
| **PL-1** | **Of all the positive and negative facts held by containing abstractions higher in the fractal, how do we reasonably determine which are *relevant* to improvisation at a local level?** **The central open question of this proposal**, and the one [C20](#recommended-order) exists to pressure. **Note three things about the framing, each load-bearing.** **(i) It spans positive facts as well as negative ones**, so it is wider than licence: a tavern authored with a roaring fire should inform local improvisation (soot, shadows, warmth) without that fact being a permission at all. **P5's licence frame covers only the negative channel**, and this question is the reason not to mistake the proposal for the whole mechanism. **(ii) *Relevant*, deliberately, and not *salient*** --- [salience is already spoken for](#narrative-integrity-is-in-service-to-reincorporation-2026-08-09), where it measures how much contradicting a fact would strain, and the two come apart (a player's *my character hates cats* is strain-expensive and irrelevant to a place setting; the tavern's napkin stock is the reverse). **Relevance is also the word [PS-4](#open-questions-p4-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused) already uses for context selection, which is correct rather than coincidental --- see (iii).** **(iii) This is the initiative's founding question arriving inside P5.** *Which ancestor facts belong in this packet* is [C3](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c3-the-snare-trap-coyote-cannot-name)'s scoping problem, AB-22's affinity filter and AB-24's ambient tier, one level in. **Reassuring** --- no new hard problem is being invented --- **and cautionary**, since P5 thereby inherits an unsolved one. **Iteration 1 elected 2026-08-09: candidate 6 (LLM judgment at mint time), in its most naive brute-force form, classified as a Prototype --- see [PL-1 iteration 1](#pl-1-iteration-1-brute-force-relevance-classified-as-a-prototype-2026-08-09) for the fit, the two cautions, and the rollback triggers.** **Electing one does not close the row:** iteration 2 is expected to be *annotate at mint time* (candidate: precomputed per ancestor-fact/entity-kind), and the remaining candidates stay live as refinements or replacements. **Candidate answers, listed to keep the space open rather than to rank them:** copy-at-import ([C19](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c19-filling-out-the-place-setting-and-the-tavern-that-ran-out-of-napkins)'s shipped-in-design mechanism, and **partial by construction** --- it says nothing about entities that never imported, which is P5 clause 10's gap); kind-based (some fact kinds containment-transitive, others local); weight-based (**AB-46**); depth- or distance-based; semantic proximity (the objects lane already carries embeddings); LLM judgment at mint time; explicit authorial declaration of reach. **The illustration to reason from, inherited 2026-08-09 from the retired C20 and sharper here than it was there.** A tavern authored **poor** containing a place setting: poverty should plausibly reach inward and stop silver flatware being improvised. A tavern **out of napkins**: [C19](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c19-filling-out-the-place-setting-and-the-tavern-that-ran-out-of-napkins) beat 5 says it need **not** reach the same place setting. **Structurally identical, and the discriminator is therefore not structural.** **What settles it is a causal story, not a rule:** *perhaps the tavern is out of napkins precisely because the last one was laid on that place setting* --- under which the napkin exists and explains the shortage. **And the opposite answer is equally fine:** judge no-napkins relevant and the setting simply lacks one. **Both produce coherent fiction, which is the strongest evidence that relevance is *reasoning* rather than retrieval** --- and therefore that the answer to this row is an [AB-19](#open-decisions-design--plan-only)-style bounded LLM step over a well-chosen packet, not a lookup table. **Expecting a rule to adjudicate poverty-versus-napkins was the wrong shape of expectation**, and it cost a falsification case to notice.

**One distinction this row now owns, promoted from P5 clause 10:** **permission is *copied* at import; context is *read live*.** That is what keeps P5 clause 6's no-cascade from conflicting with live relevance, and it is the difference between *imported-from* and *merely-inside*.

**Shipped prior art, found 2026-08-09 and it is this row's most concrete input:** [`filterTropeAffinitiesByRoom.ts`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/objects/filterTropeAffinitiesByRoom.ts) **is this question already answered, badly and on purpose** --- a container (room) narrowing which of its facts reach a contained object's reasoning, resolved by a `switch` over three hardcoded room sets. **Its answer does not generalise** (that is the *circumstantial, not structural* criticism [Coyote as prior art](#coyote-as-prior-art-bounded-llm-reasoning-that-is-not-bounded-structurally) already makes), **but its shape is exactly this row's**, and it enforces at **context-assembly** time rather than at mint time --- filter what the reasoning step is shown, and there is nothing to refuse. **Any candidate below should be checked against whether it could replace that switch.**

**One check already available against the kind-based candidate:** a fixed table of which fact kinds propagate is close to the compiled-vocabulary move **AB-19 rejected**, so it needs a fallback rather than an enumeration --- which is a real constraint on it, not a disqualification |

### PL-1 iteration 1: brute-force relevance, classified as a Prototype (2026-08-09)

**A candidate is elected rather than a new one proposed.** [PL-1](#open-questions-p5-sub-questions-ids-stable-never-reused) lists seven; **iteration 1 is candidate 6, *LLM judgment at mint time*, in its most naive form**: hand a reasoning step **every** permission restriction held above the current node in the fractal, together with the candidate improvisation, and let it judge which bear. **It works and it does not scale**, which is precisely [P4](#what-p4-being-load-bearing-here-means-2026-08-07)'s shape.

**Classified as a *Prototype*, on P4's precedent and for P4's reason.** Not a Proposal (which costs only design time to roll back) and not a Working Hypothesis. **Code will exist before iterations 2+ are designed, so a rollback means deciding what to do with shipped code that was known going in to be non-scalable** --- sunk *engineering* cost, which pulls harder than sunk reasoning ever did. **Tag dependent rows `PL-1-i1-dependent`**, and cite what they actually need, per the over-tagging warning [the tagging test](#the-tagging-test-and-why-it-is-the-point) already gives.

**One respect in which this is a *better* prototype bet than P4's, recorded because the two will be compared.** **Iteration 1's output has the same shape as any scaled version's** --- a filtered set of relevant facts --- so iteration 2 is a **drop-in replacement**, where P4's iteration 1→2 changes the scheduling architecture and is a genuine rewrite. **And iteration 2 is already named rather than sketched:** *annotate at mint time, not at reasoning time* is the first row of [the Coyote prior-art table](#coyote-as-prior-art-bounded-llm-reasoning-that-is-not-bounded-structurally), and precomputing relevance per (ancestor-fact, entity-kind) pair is exactly that pattern applied here. P4's iteration 3 is considerably vaguer.

**Two cautions, recorded now rather than discovered during the build.**

1. **Depth is *not* a problem, contrary to first impression.** CoyoteGame today is two-level, and [depth is the corpus's named blind spot](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#phase-0-corpus) --- but **a prototype that builds abstraction layers creates its own depth**: genre → room → contraption → part is four levels, [C11](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c11-the-rube-goldberg-machine)'s shape. It tests depth by construction.
2. **Volume *is* a problem, and it is the same one P4 has.** Brute force fails on the **number** of restrictions, and Coyote is a small authored world that will not accumulate many. **So iteration 1 validates the *mechanism* and not the *scaling threshold*.** Same limitation P4 iteration 1 already accepts (*workable for a prototype the size of CoyoteGame*), same mitigation: **instrument to extrapolate, not to pass.**

**What cannot be measured, stated because the P4 framing invites skipping past it.** P4 measures countable things --- fan-out, settle time, diamond frequency. **Relevance has no ground truth.** *Was that judgment right* is undecidable for the same reason [there is no wrong fiction](#what-a-fault-even-is-given-the-medium-2026-08-09). **The prototype can therefore measure cost and not quality.** The available quality proxy is **contradiction frequency**, which is [already on P4 iteration 1's instrumentation list](#what-p4-being-load-bearing-here-means-2026-08-07) --- **the two prototypes share the measurement rather than building separate machinery**, which is also a reason to build them in the same window.

**Rollback triggers, set now while they cost nothing, and stated on cost and contradiction rate --- never on accuracy.**

1. **If the *relevant fraction* does not fall materially below the total** --- if nearly every ancestor restriction is judged to bear --- **then relevance as a framing failed, not this iteration.** Filtering that filters nothing cannot be rescued by precomputing it, because there is nothing to precompute away. **This is the direct analogue of P4's *fan-out does not compress* trigger**, and it fires against PL-1 itself rather than against candidate 6.
2. **If relevance judgments are unstable** --- the same (ancestor-fact, candidate) pair judged differently across calls --- **iteration 2 is impossible**, since there is nothing stable to cache. That kills the named optimisation path specifically rather than the framing, and it is cheap to measure from day one.

**What this does to P5's status, and it is the point of doing it.** The plan previously recorded that *P5's shape claims stand alone but its utility is gated on relevance --- the initiative's founding unsolved problem*. **That is now weaker in a specific and favourable way: PL-1 is no longer open-ended, it is buildable**, with a candidate elected, an iteration 2 named, and triggers set. **P5 is blocked on a build rather than on an unsolved problem** --- a different and much better position, and the one that makes its shape clauses worth locking ahead of the utility question.

### The cost that is not optional, and it is AB-19's

**If licence is only ever authored, P5 recreates the thing AB-19 rejected.** That row killed the authored-pattern-vocabulary shortcut partly on the objection that it *"limits players to what someone anticipated"* --- and a licence system whose limits all trace to an author is that objection with a new name. **So mechanisms by which licence widens in play are load-bearing for P5's acceptability, not a later refinement.** One is already available and should not be mistaken for the whole answer: **licence governs what the *system* may invent, not what the world may become.** A player who puts a napkin on a closed place setting is not improvising --- the whole gains a member by ordinary mutation, and the licence was never in the path.

## Parked extension points this initiative would unpark

**Framing (2026-08-05, from the author of most of these):** these were laid down deliberately, in anticipation of a wider-reaching reasoning effort that would eventually need them. **This is that effort.** So unparking is the *expected shape* of implementation here, not a cost to be minimized or a sign the slice is too big --- and a candidate slice that unparks nothing is more likely mis-aimed than cheap.

Every row below was verified in this repo during the design conversation. Each is a **deliberate** deferral with a stated trigger, not an oversight.

| Parked capability | Where it is recorded | What unparks it |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **`OBJECT#` / Area membership hosts** | [`positions/AGENT.concepts.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.concepts.md#fractal-position-graphs-container-scale-and-edges) --- "deferred until needed" | Composition, if part-of resolves as a host relationship (AB-4). Nesting is the case that makes them needed. |
| **A root concept on `EphemeraPositionGraph`** | [`positionGraph/AGENT.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/positionGraph/AGENT.md#known-limitation-deferred) --- pinned in two places, with `CarryClosureFragment` set to collapse into it | AB-3. The reciprocal note exists precisely so whoever adds a root here finds it. |
| **Features as graph nodes** | Same --- node `tag` union is `'Character' \| 'Object'` only | AB-12's Room/Feature half. Note Features are designed as *narrower* participants, not Object peers. |
| **Character-relation widening (BD-36)** | Same --- `HostRelationalEdge` endpoints are object-only; `removeCharacter`'s assert is vacuously satisfied today | Relations over non-object nodes; the assert is the placeholder already installed for that day. |
| **Reverse edge-reference index / post-clear sweep** ***(most load-bearing --- three independent consumers)*** | Same --- adjacency indexes *node* placement, not edge-only references | Cross-host abstraction membership (AB-6, AB-8); part-connections referencing a licensing relation (AB-26); and the backwards discovery a licence-removing mutation needs (AB-29). Three separate lines of reasoning arrived here independently on 2026-08-05. |
| **Render-cache `n` / mark-state axis + `situationId`** | [`renderCache/AGENT.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/renderCache/AGENT.md); pinned to `[]` and `SITUATION#DEFAULT` by iteration 10 | AB-23. Situational *meaning* is this axis raised from rendering to interpretation. |
| **`thinking` `claimed` status + a claimer** | [`thinking/AGENT.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/thinking/AGENT.md); status shipped, no producer, retention already protects queued work | AB-18, AB-24 --- deferred recognition and the ambient context tier. |
| **`EMBEDDING#PERSPECTIVE#...` rows** | [`objects/AGENT.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/objects/AGENT.md) Follow-ups --- "when perspective-scoped similarity is needed" | AB-23. Perspective-scoped semantic similarity *is* situational meaning, expressed in the embedding layer. |
| **WML `Relational` tag / `EdgeList` consolidation** | [`positions/AGENT.concepts.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.concepts.md#wml-convergence-future) --- deferred pending heterogeneous room/container edge lists | AB-10, AB-15 --- authored composition patterns and authored interpretive frames. |
| **`Object Placement Drift Finding` sweep** | [`positions/AGENT.contract.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.contract.md) --- deferred; `repairObjectPlacementDrift` has no caller | AB-8, and the contract-contradiction cleanup already queued in Phase 1. |
| **Backtrack channel (BD-18)** | [`AGENT.backtrackChannel.planning.md`](../actions/AGENT.backtrackChannel.planning.md) --- iteration 4, named not started | AB-20: an LLM that proposes a wrong recognition needs a structured critique path, not a silent guess. |
| **Moving an object into or out of another object's interior graph** | Nowhere yet --- newly named 2026-08-07. `MutationKernelTransferStep`'s singular `toHostId` and `applyStepSequenceCore` were built against ordinary hosts (rooms, characters); neither has been asked whether it can source from or target a node nested inside a whole's own `positionGraph` | **AB-40(a)**, from [C13](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c13-taking-and-returning-the-fork). **Recognized, not designed:** this is not an open fork between candidate answers, it is a scoped capability H3 requires and today's kernel has never been asked to supply --- the ordinary "which of these is actually a decision" test this table's framing note applies. **Narrowed 2026-08-10 by AB-5's A-and-B framing:** an *ordinary* mint sources from a room host and does not need this; it is required for **nested** wholes and for C13's fork-taking, which is a smaller trigger than first stated. |
| **`establishRelation` carrying a containment kind** | Nowhere yet --- newly named 2026-08-10. `HostRelationalEdgeKind` is `'On' \| 'Under' \| 'Against' \| 'Custom'`, so the shipped relational step has never been asked to carry AB-4/AB-48's `PartOf` | **AB-5(A)**. Mint is `transferMembership` **plus** `establishRelation` --- members move, edges do not move with them --- so the zero-new-step-kinds claim rests on this one verification. If it refuses, one step kind is added: a cost, not a shape change. **Recognized, not designed**, on the row above's test |
| **A footprint entry for a host that does not exist at plan time** | Nowhere yet --- newly named 2026-08-10. [`computeStepSequenceFootprint`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/manipulation/kernel/computeStepSequenceFootprint.ts) computes the lock set once, up front, from a snapshot, and `MultiKeyUpdate` cannot be re-entered | **AB-5(A)**. A mint's `toHostId` is the whole being created --- **knowable**, since the planner mints the id, but absent from every graph at snapshot time. Verify rather than assume: this is the one place the op's shape meets the kernel's hardest standing constraint |

**Use this table in Phase 5.** A slice's cost is substantially "which of these does it unpark, and is that one ready to be unparked" --- several have their own prerequisites, and at least one (the backtrack channel) is a sibling initiative that would need scheduling rather than absorbing.

### Shipped code this initiative would retire

**The mirror image of the table above --- not a capability to unpark, but one to decommission.** Named here so Phase 6 (graduate the slice) doesn't rediscover it from scratch.

| Shipped mechanism | Why it's a retirement candidate | What replaces it |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Carry closure** --- `computeCarryClosure`, `classifyInteractionUnderTransfer`, `CarryClosureFragment` ([`interactionUnderTransfer.ts`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/positionGraph/expandValidate/interactionUnderTransfer.ts)) | Opened 2026-08-07 from [C14](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c14-the-tray-its-cups-and-how-many-times-it-gets-picked-up). Two sub-mechanisms, both independently found to already have H3-native equivalents: the BFS-walk-and-bundle half collapses into ephemeral-whole construction (**AB-41**); the boundary-edge classification half already reuses closely-related vocabulary (`dissolve`/`carry`/`defer` --- three outcomes, not the four this plan has been citing; see AB-28) to what ports' own boundary-edge discipline uses (AB-28's fifth independent arrival). **Not a parallel path that resembles the abstraction framework --- it is the abstraction framework, called before the framework existed.** | Ephemeral-abstraction construction (AB-41) for the bundling half; `boundaryEdgeOutcomes` directly for the classification half, once H3's whole/port machinery is what a transfer operation is built against |

**Do not act on this before H3 ships.** Carry closure is today's only working mechanism for this problem; retiring it is a Phase 6 consequence, not a prerequisite for anything earlier in this plan.

## Out of scope: parked capabilities and what unparking them would look like (2026-08-10)

**This section exists to stop a park from becoming debt *and* to stop this plan from becoming a roadmap.** A park needs enough of an exit sketch that it can be built *toward* rather than merely deferred --- but the moment those sketches become designs, this plan acquires responsibility for everything downstream of its own work, which is not a burden one task plan should carry.

**The rules that keep it small, and they are the point of the section rather than preamble:**

1. **Sketches, deliberately shallow.** An entry says *what would have to change and which lane owns it*. It does not design anything. If an entry starts acquiring open questions with IDs, it has outgrown this section and wants its own plan.
2. **Only capabilities this plan actively *parked*.** Not everything it enables, not everything it makes easier, not anything merely adjacent --- otherwise the list is unbounded by construction. **[Parked extension points](#parked-extension-points-this-initiative-would-unpark) is the section for things this initiative *unparks*; this one is its mirror.**
3. **Each entry carries the constraints the park must respect to stay exitable.** That is the half a plain "parked, revisit later" note loses, and the half that actually costs something if it is not written down now.
4. **None of this is in scope.** No phase builds it, no row owns it, and an entry here is **not** a commitment that it will ever be built.

### Rooms nesting in rooms

**Parked at [AB-12(b)](#open-decisions-design--plan-only)** --- schema-representable, modeller- and UI-unsupported, improvisation redirected to mint a **Feature `PartOf` the room** rather than a nested Room. **Unpark trigger:** a case a Feature cannot carry, or [H3 clause 8](#h3-ports-as-the-boundary-mechanism-decomposition-as-a-modellers-choice)'s apprehension scale being built for other reasons.

**What unparking would involve, and the shape of it is the reassuring part:**

| Piece | What changes | Lane |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Representation** | **Nothing.** Locked-frame clause 3 already makes a Room's graph structurally identical to any other host's, and a Room node inside a Room's node list is an ordinary containment edge | This one --- already done |
| **Existing sub-spaces** | Re-tag Feature -> Room. Same graph shape; the difference is host-kind union membership and what apprehension does with it | This one --- already done |
| **The gate** | Remove one labelled mint-site type check | This one --- one edit by construction |
| **Apprehension policy** | H3 clause 8's reserved field gets an answer for rooms: what a character in one sub-space perceives of another. **This is the actual cost of unparking** | Perception / H3 clause 8 |
| **Perception + projection** | Room-scale perception becomes sub-room-scale --- affordance compose, description, LLM context assembly | Perception lane |
| **Player-facing UI** | How a player sees, enters and navigates sub-spaces | Client |

**The result worth stating plainly: structurally the park is free to exit, and everything expensive about it belongs to other lanes.** Which means this was never really this plan's park --- it is a decision **not to hand a problem to the perception and client lanes** before they have reason to want it. That is also why it can be parked without guilt: nothing here is deferred *work of this initiative*.

**Three constraints the park must respect to stay exitable**, and these **are** in scope, because breaking them is how a cheap unpark becomes an expensive one:

1. **No code may assume a Room's graph contains no Rooms.** The park is a **policy**, not an invariant. `evaluateComplexityPreGates`' `multiPresent` error is the worked example of what ignoring this costs --- the schema always permitted multi-presence, some code decided it could not happen, and correcting it is now a five-site, player-visible obligation.
2. **The gate stays a single labelled site.** It is a type check at the mint point today; if it diffuses into scattered assumptions, the one-edit exit is gone and the exit becomes a survey.
3. **Feature sub-spaces stay structurally identical to Rooms.** Do not "optimise" Features into something narrower than a general host --- the re-tag exit depends entirely on them already being one, and this is the constraint most likely to be broken by someone with a good local reason.

## Phase 0 corpus

**Moved 2026-08-07 to [`AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md`](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md).** The corpus grew to ten cases and ~270 lines, and it is the block most often read on its own and least often edited --- so it lives beside this plan rather than inside it, per [`taskPlanning/AGENT.md`](../../../../AGENT.md)'s guidance on keeping bulk out of a plan body. **It is not archived and not optional reading:** Phases 1 and 2 argue from it directly, and its per-case annotation tallies are their input.

Cases to date: C1 (Room/Feature participation), C2 (the rope refused at the gate), C3 (the snare Coyote cannot name), C4 (the Faerie Court place setting), C5 (the chain mounted at both ends), C6 (the impromptu timer), C7 (Ariadne's thread), C8 (the flashlight that vanished), C9 (coiling the rope back in), C10 (the moonbase computer system), C11 (the Rube Goldberg machine), C12 (the moonbase power system and the cut cable), C13 (taking and returning the fork). Remaining cases are listed under [Phase 0](#recommended-order).
