| **Corpus retro-fitted to the axes (C1--C11)** --- tally in one place rather than per case; **zero cases primary on authoring**, the largest measured hole; positioning-primary cases confirmed as the ones that discriminated least; C11 found to bite by **internal contradiction**, a mode no axis covers | Done (2026-08-07) |
| **C11 (Rube Goldberg machine) written** --- the corpus's first case with more than two levels, built to challenge an assessment made hours earlier; **H1 clause 1a falsified on a single object** (`StringSpan` is a part and a whole at once, so 1a and multi-host wholes are jointly unsatisfiable) and its *decompose further* justification found to have **no base case**; decomposition demoted from correctness requirement to modelling convenience; **PQ-13 promoted to the fractal's base case**; the `multiPresent`-gate reprieve claimed the same day **withdrawn**, so P1's five-code-sites argument is back in force; *position*-relativity recorded as a second and harder challenge to apprehension scale; locked frame explicitly **not** re-opened, but whole/part clarified as roles | Done (2026-08-07) |
| **Depth named as the corpus's blind spot** --- C1--C10 are all two-level, which is how a disjoint-kinds assumption survived two days unchallenged | Done (2026-08-07) |
| **"Mystery is the degenerate zero-stakes case of reconciliation" withdrawn --- it described a floor as a ceiling.** That framing read a mystery as **deferral** (reconciliation postponed). **A first-class mystery object caps a *minimum effort*, not a maximum time:** *this is unknown and **will not be revealed** before a certain amount of player investment*. **The floor is the whole construct** --- a mystery whose answer is available on request is worthless, and the locked room would be solved by asking. **Three repairs.** **(i) A mystery *is* a manner narrowing applied to something not yet established**, so C20's clause-2 split reaches the case that motivated it and **no second construct is needed**. **(ii) It *creates* stakes rather than lacking them** --- *this is worth something and it will cost you* --- which is the precise inversion the withdrawn phrasing made. **(iii) "Or ignored" stops needing separate justification:** under deferral it is a dangling loose end, under a floor it is simply **nobody paid the price**, a complete outcome. **And it makes the construct mechanically real**, as *first-class object* was already asserting --- a floor is a **predicate a gate can evaluate**, where deferred reconciliation is not checkable at all. **AB-46 extended symmetrically:** weight runs **forward as effort-to-establish** for the not-yet-established and **backward as effort-to-overturn** for the established --- one quantity over the whole lifecycle rather than half of it | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **Mystery's precondition named: it is cheap only where nobody is vested.** Registering *I wonder what's going on there* **defers** reconciliation, and deferral is free exactly when no one is waiting on the answer --- so a contradiction touching a **vested** contribution is not an intriguing loose end but an **unresolved social tension**, injuring the reincorporation that narrative integrity exists to protect. **The provenance split predicts the whole gradient**: system-improvised against itself is trivial, system-improvised against authored or player-driven is asymmetric and easy, authored against player-driven is AB-29's asset-added branch, and **player-driven against player-driven is hardest --- the one case the mystery move does not defuse**, since both contributors are waiting. **Two consequences.** **AB-46 gains a third job** (weight decides **deferrability**, after strain and effort-to-overturn) --- **three independent uses on one scalar, which is evidence the quantity is real** rather than invented per problem. **And the feedback-regime worry narrows favourably:** in this system the GM **is** the LLM, so machine-generated fiction is **system-improvised and therefore lowest-weight by construction** --- the contradictions an LLM in the loop produces are overwhelmingly the absorbable kind, while the expensive ones are player-against-player, which the LLM does not author. **Closing refinement: *mystery* is the degenerate zero-stakes case of reconciliation, not a separate mechanism** --- where deferral is unaffordable the craft move is to find the reading in which **both** contributions stand, performed rather than promised, with AB-47's out-of-character table channel as the fallback | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **Contradiction handling named as a *mechanism*, not a disposition --- fiction registers a mystery.** Two flatly contradictory facts (*Ozymand died twenty thousand years ago* / *Ozymand fought Grimhond two hundred years ago*) are handled by a play-to-find-out GM as **"Right? Sounds impossible. I wonder what's going on there"** --- **neither fact retracted**, the contradiction registered as a mystery, solved through play **or ignored**. **Three consequences.** **(i) No retraction required --- fourth arrival of this plan's *not a knowledge base* theme**, and the second independent route to *do not retract* after P4's dataflow reframe found the same thing for causal state; the graph holds **established fiction**, not asserted truths. **(ii) This is C20's mystery construct generalised**, reached from the opposite input: *nothing obvious* converts an **unknown** into a mystery, Ozymand converts a **contradiction** into one, and the downstream object is identical --- so **mystery is the system's general defer-with-interest primitive**, needing no new state, being a **decorated not-established** that inherits C17's *settled, not pending*. **(iii) "Or ignored" is load-bearing** --- a registered mystery carries **no obligation**, which is why it costs one sentence and why it does not import PS-10's unbounded-worklist problem. **Prior art named: *play to find out* (Apocalypse World), the second load-bearing frame this plan has taken from tabletop and theatre craft rather than computing**, after Johnstone's reincorporation --- a pattern worth noticing in where the useful answers keep coming from. **Limit preserved: contradictory *fiction* absorbs, contradictory *machinery* does not** --- a gate must return yes or no, so a licence that both permits and forbids needs a **tie-break** (fail-safe: refuse), which weakens the paradox re-opener further without dissolving it | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **P5's shape clauses locked, and a proposal-level rollback trigger set --- P5 is prototype-ready.** **Locked: 1, 2 (in its repaired content/manner form), 3 and 7**, each corpus-grounded rather than argued, on the parts-and-ports precedent. **Enumerated loudly as NOT locked:** representation (AB-42), composition of nested licences, narrative weight (AB-46), **transfer-is-copy** (P5 clause 6 --- *policy with a trigger*, a different category that must not acquire lock status by proximity), licence-as-setting-character, and **AB-47's genre boundaries, which are a different construct entirely**. **Three consequences owned rather than deferred:** manner narrowings need judgment to *apply*, so AB-43 has no purely descriptive answer for some regions; only the **shape** is locked, with utility still gated on PL-1 iteration 1; and **locking ahead of a killing test is deliberate**, mitigated by the trigger rather than by a claim that no such test exists. **Trigger: if licence records prove reconstructible from ordinary established facts with no distinguishing behaviour, licence is not a construct and P5 failed** --- measurable from iteration 1, firing against the proposal rather than the implementation. **Second re-opener named so it is on the record as considered: a true internal contradiction (C11's mode), for which no candidate scenario exists.** **And its threat is bounded by the medium rather than by discipline:** a deductive KR fears paradox because of **explosion**, and this system does **no deductive closure** --- a contradiction sits there being odd instead of corrupting inference. Under the feedback-regime frame it is **maximally amplifying in a deductive system and damping in a narration system** (third time that frame has predicted a result ahead of the argument), fiction is authored *toward* coherence where simulated reality is not, and adversarial paradox-construction is handled by the **mint gate refusing to reason about it** rather than by consistency maintenance. **Bound restated: a paradox producing a *structural* defect is still a bug** | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **C20 written --- the locked room, and *nothing obvious*.** P5's first falsification case lands after **two re-aimings, both of which retired targets that were this plan's errors rather than P5's defects**. **P5 clause 2 falsified in an assumption it never stated:** every worked negative had been **extensional** (*no deep fryer*, *no napkins*), while *no **obvious** entrances* is a **predicate requiring evaluation** --- repaired by splitting narrowings into **content** (a set that may not be minted) and **manner** (a way of minting that is not permitted). **P5 clause 1 vindicated rather than attacked** --- a locked-room mystery needs no closed world, only a high-weight manner narrowing, so the three-value model survives whole. **AB-43 materially worse** (some regions are predicates, not descriptions) and **a second judgment point** opened beside PL-1's. **A bound recorded:** no authorial control over the **path** --- a puzzle solvable only by its author's envisioned solution is AB-19's rejection at world scale, and indie RPG practice supplies the empirical half --- **disambiguated from AB-47**, which legitimately controls the **palette**. **The GM technique the case turns on is the finding that generalises:** answering *nothing obvious* is the narrowing **narrated**, a **third response mode and the only in-fiction one**, which converts an **unknown** into a **mystery** --- a positive mint rather than a withholding --- and **commits to a shape of future answer without requiring the answer to exist yet**, which is why it costs one sentence and why the model should support it directly rather than forcing an author to pre-place the solution. **Pattern flagged before the next falsification case is queued: three targets, only the third was the proposal's** | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **PL-1 iteration 1 elected and classified as a Prototype --- relevance becomes buildable, and P5's status question changes shape.** **Candidate 6 of seven (*LLM judgment at mint time*) elected in its most naive brute-force form:** hand a reasoning step every restriction held above the current node plus the candidate improvisation, and let it judge which bear. Works, does not scale --- **P4's shape exactly**, and classified **Prototype** for P4's reason (code before later iterations are designed, so rollback costs engineering). Tag `PL-1-i1-dependent`. **One respect better than P4's bet:** iteration 1's output shape equals any scaled version's, so **iteration 2 is a drop-in replacement** rather than P4's architecture rewrite --- and iteration 2 is **already named** rather than sketched, being *annotate at mint time, not at reasoning time*, the first row of the Coyote prior-art table. **Two cautions recorded rather than discovered:** depth is **not** a problem (a prototype building abstraction layers creates four levels by construction, C11's shape) but **volume is**, identically to P4 --- so iteration 1 validates the **mechanism, not the scaling threshold**, and must instrument to extrapolate rather than to pass. **What cannot be measured, named so the P4 framing does not carry us past it: relevance has no ground truth**, so the prototype measures **cost, not quality**; the quality proxy is **contradiction frequency**, already on P4 iteration 1's list, so the two prototypes **share the measurement**. **Rollback triggers set on cost and contradiction rate, never accuracy:** if the relevant fraction does not fall materially below the total then **relevance as a framing failed** (filtering that filters nothing cannot be rescued by precomputing it --- the direct analogue of P4's *fan-out does not compress*), and if judgments are **unstable** across calls then iteration 2 is impossible, since nothing is cacheable. **Status consequence: P5 is blocked on a build, not on an unsolved problem**, which is the condition that makes locking its shape clauses ahead of its utility honest rather than premature | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **Coyote mapping corrected the same day: a closed *reasoning vocabulary*, not a closed *world*.** Prompted by checking what is actually in the enum against what is in a desert. **`long-fall` is not a desert noun** --- it is not an object anyone finds --- and **lizards and sand are desert-justified yet excluded**, so the five entries are the standard **gag affordances**: the vocabulary was drawn around **what the reasoning step needs**, not what the setting contains. **`affordancesProvided` proves the point from the open side** --- `{ object: 'lizard', roles: ['Bait'] }` is representable today, so the closed enum constrains the **environment as a source**, not the fiction. **Two claims from the row below are therefore withdrawn or downgraded.** *Withdrawn:* P5 clause 1 is **not** falsified --- `filterTropeAffinitiesByRoom` says *do not reason with a rock wall here*, not *there is no rock wall here*, and the two coincide only because nothing else in a five-room demo surfaces environment objects, which is the **circumstantial-not-structural** criticism arriving against a claim this plan had just made itself. *Downgraded:* P5 clause 3's inward reach has a shipped **analogue**, not an instance --- what the room narrows is reasoning inputs, not licence. **Root cause named: Coyote is a *reasoning-axis* system and P5 an *authoring-axis* proposal**, and the first draft mapped one onto the other without declaring the seam. **The mapping stands where it belongs** --- PL-1, the reasoning-axis question inside P5 --- and **C20 is not pre-answered**: its question is intact and sharper, *does a closed reasoning vocabulary license a closed world?* | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **Coyote's two affordance branches mapped onto P5 --- shipped prior art, and one falsification.** `environmentAffordances` (**closed enum**, supplied by the **location**, filtered by [`filterTropeAffinitiesByRoom.ts`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/objects/filterTropeAffinitiesByRoom.ts)) and `affordancesProvided` (**open string**, supplied by the **item**, explicitly unfiltered) turn out to be the **container** side and the **contributor** side of improvisation permission, already running. **Four confirmations:** P5 clause 3's **inward reach is shipped** --- a room narrowing what a staged object may claim, which is the thing this plan spent a day mistakenly calling a contradiction; **PL-1 is shipped in hardcoded form** (a `switch` over three room sets), the **seventh** pre-registration this initiative has found and the first of its *central open question* rather than a mechanism; enforcement happens at **context-assembly** time --- a **third** enforcement point beside AB-47's mint-time and rejected narration-time, and probably the best, since **what is not in the packet cannot be proposed and needs no refusal message**; and `aptness`'s `COYOTE_AFFINITY_APTNESS_MIN = 0.2` floor is a shipped **relevance threshold**. `intended?: true` flagged as a candidate two-valued AB-46 weight, **not asserted** --- consumers untraced. **The falsification: P5 clause 1 is wrong for closed vocabularies, and shipped code says so.** Environment affordances are closed-world (silence means **no**) and that is **correct**, because the vocabulary is five authored items --- so **the three-value model collapses to two whenever the vocabulary is genuinely enumerable**, and P5 clause 1 is now scoped to **open domains**. C20 is not retired by this (it asks whether an author may close an *open* domain by fiat, which Coyote never faces) but now **starts from a worked example**. **One principle falls out of the vocabulary asymmetry:** the constraining side is closed and the contributing side open because **a constraint needs a decidable membership test and a contribution does not** --- which is AB-43's difficulty restated as a property rather than a puzzle | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **C20 retired as posed and re-aimed at P5 clause 1; P5 clause 3 reworded; the tavern demoted to a PL-1 illustration.** **The contradiction C20 was queued to exploit was this plan's drafting error, not P5's defect:** P5 clause 3 said a limit reaches *no further* than its holder **without saying in which direction**, and containment scope is directional --- a place setting's limit reaching the tavern is **outside** its holder-scope, the tavern's reaching **inward** is inside it. **No contradiction, and C11's internal-contradiction mode was mis-claimed.** P5 clause 3 now reads *an entity's scope includes what it contains*, permitting inward reach while leaving **which** facts reach to PL-1: **the clause permits, relevance selects.** **P5 clause 10 resolved rather than falsified**, and usefully --- **permission is *copied* at import; context is *read live***, which names the missing *imported-from* versus *merely-inside* distinction and stops P5 clause 6's no-cascade conflicting with live relevance. **C20 re-aimed at P5 clause 1** (unbounded by default) via the **tightly-authored closed world**, where an author wants silence to mean *no* --- **independent of relevance**, which the tavern version was not, and whose obvious repair (zero the root, widen below) forces the unworked composition semantics. **The tavern survives as PL-1's illustration**, where it does real work: *poor* and *out of napkins* are structurally identical with different required answers, and what settles them is a **causal story** (*perhaps the tavern is out of napkins because the last one went onto that very place setting*) --- **either answer is coherent fiction, which is the evidence that relevance is reasoning rather than retrieval**, and therefore an AB-19-style bounded LLM step rather than a lookup. **Expecting a rule to adjudicate it was the wrong shape of expectation, and it cost a falsification case to notice.** **Structural finding recorded with it: P5's shape claims stand alone, but P5's *utility* is gated on relevance** --- the initiative's founding unsolved problem --- so P5 can be locked at the shape level and cannot be evaluated for usefulness ahead of PL-1 | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **AB-47 settled --- two constructs, one enforcement point --- after reversing twice in one day.** The one-construct reversal below was itself overturned by an argument neither earlier draft had made: **unify genre boundaries with weighted world facts as *a licence narrowing with weight infinite*, and six consumers each become a branch on whether the weight is infinite** --- mint gate, out-of-character response text, escalation channel, reincorporation weighting, write authority, moderation signal. **Six for six, sharing only a storage shape.** A sentinel that changes behaviour at every read site is **two constructs with extra steps**, and the *one construct with a discriminating field* compromise collapses identically, a field determining every behaviour being a type distinction spelled as an enum. **Stronger than the argument AB-47 opened on**, which depended on someone eventually erring; this one holds under perfect discipline. **The concrete difference is the pair of out-of-character responses:** *persuade other players, outside the fiction* versus *nobody has ever done this --- it is possible, but a long-term quest*. **Draft 2's error recorded because its shape recurs: an objection falling is not a conclusion arriving** (the mint-gate finding removed one of two objections and was treated as making the case) --- the mirror of this plan's standing warning that an unacted-on caveat becomes an assumption. **Two spoils: AB-46 gains semantics** (*weight is the effort required to overturn*, symmetric with investment-behind) **and is bounded away from genre boundaries** (not on the scale at all, so weight is not a universal currency with an infinite value); **and the legibility caution is discharged**, both constructs now having a legible out-of-character response, with the weighted one converting refusal into a **quest hook**. **What survives from the reversal is its durable half: one enforcement point** --- a mint-time gate suffices for both, neither needs a narration-time twin | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **AB-47's lean reversed the day it was opened --- one construct, not two.** Both arguments for a type split fell to one observation: **a mint-time gate is sufficient.** Players cannot be stopped from assembling a tube, powder and shot and asking for a gun; the system can **refuse to mint the abstraction** and thereafter **steadily refuse to reason about it**. **The narration objection was answered by re-scoping, not rebuttal** --- gating structure and tolerating narration drift *is* the integrity->prevention / coherence->damping split, applied where it was already committed; the gate catches the load-bearing minted object and lets a stray NPC line through, which is the correct priority under a **blast-radius** frame rather than a prevention one. **Refusal is structurally silent**: an unminted abstraction produces no packet, so nothing has to obey anything --- **bounded context doing a third job**, after scalability and fault isolation. **The unrepresentability argument shrank from *needs a type* to *needs a field*.** **What survives is write authority** --- social contracts change by out-of-character table negotiation, plausibly a fourth provenance that separates from *authored* only in multiplayer. **Two cautions carried:** silent refusal is safe but not legible (a communication problem, and it cannot be explained in-world), and blast-radius reduction assumes moderators arrive. **Out of scope by agreement:** the table's social contract and moderation practice. **Kept in scope because the mechanism yields it free:** refusal events are already computed, and near-miss licensing makes **approach** detection graded rather than binary --- automated instrumentation of players nearing a genre break would be novel for this class of game, where moderation has been reactive | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **C21 written and AB-47 opened --- the constraint that may not belong in the fiction at all.** *"A fantasy setting in which firearms have not been invented"* and *"a fantasy setting, without firearms"* exclude identically and behave differently on four probes; the decisive one is **defeasibility** --- a player who researches gunpowder should succeed under the first, which locates the constraint **inside** the diegesis, and pushes on the genre boundary under the second, which locates it outside. **Candidate framing: a bare genre-forbid is a *social contract*, not a fictional contribution** --- inviolable rather than weighted (**the first construct in this design that is not a matter of degree**, which bounds AB-46), breached rather than strained, owed no diegetic explanation, and constraining **narration** rather than only world-state. **Unification recorded and not waved off** (a root-level licence with weight infinite absorbs three of four consequences; the narration one does not reduce), with the stronger objection being **type separation makes the category error unrepresentable rather than forbidden** --- C18 Finding 4's move on its **fourth** arrival. **Third instance of the corpus's identical-surface/different-behaviour family** after C3 and C18, and each time the missing thing was *how it came to be that way*. **Scale-invariance checked and passes** (*poor* versus *war-stripped* at tavern scale), so it feeds C20 at no cost. P5 clause 2 shown to flatten the causal form, P5 clause 8 put under pressure (a fact entailing a licence state means **something propagated**), P5 clause 9 given real content | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **PL-1 opened, and C20's predicted repair withdrawn before the case was written.** A queued falsification case carrying a named expected answer gets written **to** that answer, so the prediction (character-limits transitive, inventory-limits local) was struck and **replaced with the question**: *of all the positive and negative facts held by containing abstractions higher in the fractal, how do we reasonably determine which are **relevant** to improvisation at a local level?* **Three properties of that framing are load-bearing.** It spans **positive** facts, so it does not presuppose licence is the only channel ancestor context travels --- **which means P5's frame covers one channel of two**. It says **relevant**, not *salient*, avoiding a collision with the strain measure (the two come apart in both directions) and correctly matching the word PS-4 already uses. And it is **the initiative's founding question --- C3/AB-22/AB-24's context-scoping problem --- arriving one level in**, which is reassuring (no new hard problem) and cautionary (P5 inherits an unsolved one). Seven candidates listed unranked, with copy-at-import noted as **partial by construction**; the withdrawn prediction gains a strike it did not have when made, since a fixed propagation table is close to the compiled-vocabulary move **AB-19 rejected**. Licence composition folded in as PL-1's licence-shaped half | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **P5 decomposed into 11 numbered clauses, and C20 queued as its first falsification case.** Numbered **before** the case rather than after, on H3's precedent, so a failure lands on a clause rather than on the proposal. **The exercise paid immediately: four claims P5 was already relying on had never been stated** --- clause **9** (a licence is a *shaped space*, not a scalar --- *warm but poor* cannot ride a single dial), clause **10** (entities not related by import have no licence relationship, which leaves an authored place setting unrelated to its container), clause **11** (authoring sets licence unattenuated), and a correction to clause **7**: C18 stated the discriminator as *intensional -> open*, but a **poor** tavern is intensional and **narrow**, so intensionality settles whether a licence exists and what **shape** it has, not whether it is open. **An unstated claim cannot be falsified**, so all four were untestable until numbering. **P5 clause 3 recorded as under direct attack** --- C20 requires it to both not-reach outward and reach inward, C11's internal-contradiction mode, and sharpest beside C19 beat 4 where the same structure demands the opposite answer. **P5's status decision is explicitly blocked on C20**, rather than promoted on the strength of three supporting cases | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **Narrative integrity redefined as service to *reincorporation*, and AB-46 opened.** In a story game, integrity protects **participant contributions being built on** rather than internal consistency as such --- so a tavern flipping well-stocked to poor is near-costless when the system improvised both, and expensive when an author wrote it or players **worked in play** to stock it. **Supersedes salience as the criterion and demotes it to a proxy** (*salience measures attention; contribution measures investment*), and **replaces the licence-clustering heuristic with an exact structural signal that already exists**: the authored / player-driven / system-improvised split in storage. **Third dent in AB-14's "provenance is invisible downstream," and restated rather than patched** --- invisible in the *merged view*, fully recoverable from *storage*; only the first was ever argued. **Division of labour clarified:** reincorporation covers authored and player commitments, tone, genre and stakes; structural well-formedness covers the reality illusion; neither covers the ground alone. **First load-bearing frame in this plan taken from theatre craft** (Johnstone's reincorporation) rather than from computing. **AB-46 records narrative weight** --- carried *on* the record as a value, not a pointer, so it composes with P5's no-cascade rather than reintroducing the provenance graph that decision declined | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **"Salience is not structural" withdrawn the same day it was written** --- challenged on the grounds that **a scene about the tavern's poverty goes hand in hand with minting licence restrictions**, so the salient and incidental cases *do* differ structurally: poverty **is** a closed licence, while the incidental case has no record at all. **Salience leaves traces, and the primary trace is licence being touched.** Recorded as a **correlate, not an identity**, with counterexamples in both directions (a scene about generosity is salient and may only *widen* licence, or touch none; C19 beat 4 narrows licence and matters to nobody). Three candidate signals named, none assessed --- licence touched at all, **clustering** of related changes (the most promising and cheapest), heat at establishment. **Makes the damping rule cheaper to act on**, since *where the story has invested* becomes partly readable. **One speculative consequence flagged for testing:** if a place's character is largely the shape of its licence, then **licence is the generative half of setting character**, which is a far larger claim than P5 currently makes | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **"Fault" redefined for the medium, and the terminology collision it exposed fixed.** A fault is **not** *the wrong fiction* --- **there is no wrong fiction** --- it is **something that strains the integrity of the story.** Two consequences: **accuracy is the wrong standard**, so validating against truth is the wrong machinery (what is checkable is consistency with what the fiction already committed to, a far smaller obligation); and **strain tracks salience**, so the damping rule sharpens from *damp faults* to **damp faults in proportion to salience** --- global consistency was never the target. **A third job for narrative heat**, fed to PS-4 with that row's own warning attached rather than treated as answered. **Terminology fixed rather than absorbed:** *integrity* was doing two jobs, now split into **narrative integrity** (the graded measure), **structural well-formedness** (non-negotiable), and **coherence** (one component of the first, not a synonym). **Structural defects re-justified under the single measure** as *latent strain* --- they cash out later as the world failing to behave like a world --- rather than as a category exempt from it. **Negative statement extended:** *no wrong fiction* **relocates** the standard rather than removing it, since it is quotable in exactly the way the section title is | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **The feedback regime named as the thing to measure, and as a design principle for the system generally.** Fault tolerance under LLM-in-the-loop has two regimes --- **damping** (faults corrected or papered over as the story proceeds; fine) and **amplifying** (faults spawning larger faults; problematic) --- and **the rule adopted is to seek solutions promoting the damping regime rather than solutions that leave amplification in place and try to prevent error entirely.** Mapped onto the existing line: **integrity -> prevention** (keep the gates and asserts), **coherence -> damping**. Four dampers identified, two of them **new arguments for existing mechanisms** (coarsening as a low-pass filter, so the port gains a *fourth* argument on a fourth axis; and bounded context as fault isolation, making the scalability and stability theses one mechanism). Three amplifiers named, **the first being the Abstraction Fractal's own no-privileged-level commitment** --- arbitrary-depth summaries-of-summaries --- with P4 cycles and licence widened on improvised grounds after it. **Declined as a sixth axis** on the grounds that it is a different *kind* of question (dynamics of a design, not a value the graph serves). **P4 iteration 1 gains a fourth instrumented output: fault propagation**, via contradiction frequency, summarization-chain depth, and whether contradiction rises with world age. Flagged as a **graduation candidate**, held back because nothing is built | Done (2026-08-09) |
| **Fiction recorded as a fault-tolerant medium, as a standing frame** with three existing consumers (coarsening, PS-5's glitch policy, P5's no-cascade) rather than a clause inside any one of them. **Some player-visible contradiction is fine** --- a property of the medium, not a concession, and every alternative to accepting it (refuse to improvise / trace everything / block on settling) costs more. **The useful half is the line: integrity versus coherence** --- structural well-formedness is non-negotiable, story consistency is negotiable, and P5 spends only the second. **Recorded with its negative statement on AB-0's precedent**, since the phrase will be quoted to excuse defects: it does not cover integrity failures, does not cover a contradiction a player **acted on** (agency undermined, not flavour), and **does not obviously extend to LLM context**, whose tolerance is a second and unmeasured thing. **General rule adopted: set re-open triggers at *consequence*, not detectability** --- and P5's trigger corrected accordingly the same day it was written | Done (2026-08-09) |

Re-open trigger set at **player-visible** contradiction, explicitly not at theoretical incoherence | Done (2026-08-09) |

Consistency check run and passed: *not established* still costs nothing. **AB-44 survives narrower and better-defined** --- licence-closure universal, causal force variable, with authoring the exception preventing collapse. **One cost named as non-optional:** if licence is only ever authored, P5 recreates AB-19's *limits players to what someone anticipated* objection, so widening-in-play mechanisms are load-bearing rather than a later refinement | Done (2026-08-09) |

| **Constraint provenance named as a distinction, beside requirement-versus-representation.** **The question that produced it: what *is* single-use, as a requirement?** Checked against the record rather than assumed --- and it is **not derived**: no corpus case demands it, and the plan nowhere argues it beats fan-out. It entered as a **clarification of a misreading** (*"that reading is wrong"* --- a statement about what was **meant**, not about what would work), and both of its justifications are written as **consequences** of that clarification rather than grounds for it. **So: a *minimal primitive whose justifications were discovered post-hoc*.** **The operative consequence: the absence of fan-out is not a decision against fan-out, it is the absence of a decision about it** --- so re-opening does the reasoning for the first time instead of overturning any, which is far cheaper than re-opening something like H1's clause 1b where there was an argument to defeat. **Generalized into a three-way table at the head of Open decisions:** derived constraint (corpus forces it; costliest to re-open), minimal primitive (never compared; cheapest), placeholder (known-inadequate, owes a design). **Why it earns a place: this is the mechanism by which *provisional, acted on as settled* actually happens** --- a minimal primitive accumulates post-hoc justifications until it reads as derived, then defends itself with reasoning that was never the reason. **The tell, and single-use exhibits it:** both justifications plausibly **survive** the relaxation they are cited against, and a justification that survives removal of the thing it justifies was not doing the work. **PQ-9 flagged as a likely second instance.** **Diagnostic recorded:** ask what evidence produced a constraint, not what arguments now support it | Done (2026-08-10) |
| **PQ-14 opened --- single-use re-opened, not withdrawn.** **The first time the locked-with-a-lower-bar threshold has actually been met:** single-use was *re-openable on a case rather than a preference*, and C22 is a case. **The pressure's character is what earns it a row:** the fan-out is quadratic in world size and exponential in depth, and it shows up **under quite ordinary fiction** --- a boat in a bay with a few landmarks --- not at a contrived extreme. **A scalability risk reachable by normal play is a different class of objection from one reachable only by contrivance**, and the not-locked list had not anticipated it, having said *"nothing currently open depends on their being wrong."* Something now does. **Two findings recorded so the question is worked in the right order rather than as a blanket yes/no.** **(i) The relaxation may be much narrower than the prohibition** --- single-use's two payoffs (port list as reverse index, plan-time footprint) rest on one **egress**, not one interior edge, so interior fan-in may leave both intact; **what it actually threatens is port-as-unit-of-lifecycle**, giving the candidate condition ***interior edges may share a port only where they share a lifecycle*** (true for boat-level visibility, false in general). **(ii) A modelling answer may moot it** --- originate the `Shown` edge where visibility is actually determined (the boat, not each room) and `R x L` collapses to `L` with single-use untouched, at the cost of one traversal hop. **Sequenced (ii) before (i)**, since a modelling convention that dissolves the pressure is cheaper than re-opening a locked property, and both against P4-i1 measurements rather than argument. **The scale-boundary reading keeps its previous standing** --- only single-use moved | Done (2026-08-10) |

they are **one thing at two levels of the containment hierarchy**, and the lighthouse is fully manipulable at its own. **Scale-relative truth misread as a kind distinction --- the same error as whole-and-part-as-types, made the same day three instances of it were written up**, and recorded rather than dropped because it will read as true to the next reader. **Four invariants break for the lighthouse today** (no kind fits; relations are intra-host and `applyStepSequenceCore` throws; Areas neither move nor nest movably; **nothing dissolves by a continuously changing quantity** --- every existing dissolution is action-triggered). **It bounds AB-41's new clause:** that guard was reasoned from a *distal* exemplar, and an *integral* Feature must travel with its whole, so it has to bite on ephemeral absorption and not on containment-carry. **The candidate answer is a port chain through the nesting**, filed under P3 rather than as a new proposal since it is ports doing their declared job across several boundaries --- fitting locked-frame clause 2, port-as-scale-boundary, the summarization seam, AB-17/AB-31 rebinding (the boat relocates, the egress **rebinds** where the lighthouse is also present), and P4's stored-and-settled model. **Its real risk is costed rather than gestured at: port fan-out is quadratic in world size and *exponential in depth*** --- single-use means no fan-in, so R rooms x L landmarks needs R x L ports on the boat's boundary alone, a boundary splitting k from N-k carries up to N squared over 4, and a hierarchy of branching *b* and depth *D* drives pairs as *b^2D*. **Nesting is the multiplier, which is why the cost arrives with C22 and not with any earlier port case.** **Mitigation named, not designed: relax single-use for perceptual kinds** --- coarsening to one shared boat-level port is fan-in, so coarsening and relaxation are one change seen from two sides. **The register predicted exactly this** (soundness is a property of the kind; single-use is on its re-check list), and single-use is locked-with-a-lower-bar, re-openable on a case --- which C22 is | Done (2026-08-10) |
| **The Feature narrowing turns out to be an affordance rule, and its guard moves to AB-41.** **The misread, and it was mine to catch:** *Features are never the subject of a relation* was inherited as a constraint on **edge legality**, when the source states it as participation in *"only a **subset** of the **instruction structure**"* --- alongside *look-at-able* and *not `transferMembership`-able*. **It exists so scenery can be described without the engine offering to put a mountain in a backpack.** A restriction was generalized without checking what it was drawn against. **Consequences:** the *contradiction* AB-12 and AB-30 both recorded is **withdrawn** --- Feature-as-`PartOf`-subject contradicts nothing, and the wall needs no exception because the graph layer imposes no Feature restriction at all. **But the guard is still worth having, and the right lever is the one AB-41 was already building:** *"features move only with their whole"* is defeated by **wrapping an ephemeral whole around a feature, moving it, and dissolving it** --- a mechanical exploit, since ephemeral wholes are minted per-operation by the engine while durable ones are authorial acts. **So: an ephemeral whole may not absorb a Feature**, behaving as a **boundary** (`boundaryEdgeOutcomes`) rather than an error, at the single site AB-41 collapses carry closure into. **Two representation-layer rules considered and both declined for guarding the wrong layer:** (A) *no spatial subjecthood* --- faithful to `computeCarryClosure` but contingent on the transfer table and restricting edges to prevent an operation; (B) *subjects only toward Room/Area/Feature* --- **lossy, and wrong on first contact**, since mobility is per-instance not per-kind and a ship's cabin is a moving `Room`. **A correction to my own accounting recorded with them:** both carry the *same shape* of dirt (a partition plus a rule naming one component kind), so counting enumerations discriminated nothing and *"A adds no kind enumeration"* was false --- **the clean/dirty test is fidelity, not quantity.** **Doc-correction booked** for `positionGraph/AGENT.md` on AB-8's precedent. **Second hinge left open and explicitly not closed by this:** an LLM could improvise a **durable** whole around the mountain, which no ephemeral gate catches | Done (2026-08-10) |

| **H1 clause 1b: decide whether H1 is withdrawn** | ~~Not started --- pressure tallied from four directions, rollback set measured at six rows; 1a and H1 clause 3 survive either way, so the live question is 1b + entailments. **Re-framed 2026-08-06 by [C10](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c10-the-moonbase-computer-system) before being decided: the (a)/(b) hosting fork may never have been one global question.** Not a fifth pressure --- it questions the shape of the choice rather than pushing a side. Read C10 first~~ **Done (2026-08-07) --- and decided against 1b, but not by the pressure this row was tracking.** The four tallied pressures were never the decider: [C11](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c11-the-rube-goldberg-machine) falsified **H1 clause 1a** on a single object, and with no clause of H1 clause 1 surviving, 1b's entailments (H1 clauses 4 and 5) fell with the conjunction rather than being individually defeated. **So this milestone was overtaken rather than answered** --- it completed by its premise dissolving, which a Status cell has no vocabulary for, and that is plausibly why it read *Not started* for three days after the fact. **C10's re-framing survives the withdrawal** and is the part still worth reading: the (a)/(b) hosting fork may never have been one global question. **Status corrected 2026-08-10** |
| AB-36 --- reverse edge-reference index; blocks P3 and carries a shipped guarantee (PQ-7) | Not started --- four consumers identified, argument recorded so it is not re-derived |
| **AB-32 --- collect the composition requirements; blocks AB-4, which blocks H1 clause 4/5 testing** | In progress --- three requirements answered (wholes nest; same reference mechanism; relations attach to parts without part-nodes); **its decider needs re-testing under P2** |
| **AB-33 split into requirements (AB-48) and representation, and re-scoped from a widening to a re-typing** | **Done (2026-08-09).** The row was argued **entirely** against current code --- *chosen shape rather than an oversight*, *only one is cheap*, *defect verified against code* --- with **no axis named and no requirements twin**, which is why it sat Not started for three days. **The error named: mutation is the only axis it argued on, and mutation vetoes rather than ranks**, so its cost analysis eliminates the global-edge-space reading and can never recommend the status quo. **The clean/dirty test, never applied, run and decisive:** `hostId` is doing **two jobs with opposite verdicts** --- legitimate dirt as *which node is the root* (derivable by traversal, stored faster), drift as *which collection this graph belongs to* (P2's phantom). **Two rules extracted from the run:** *deliberate is not dirt*, and *the unit of the test is the job, not the construct*. **The re-scope is the practical outcome:** the cheap reading does not remove `hostId`, it renames and re-points it --- `EphemeraPositionGraphData {hostId, nodes, edges?}` and `CarryClosureFragment {rootId, members, edges}` are the same three-field shape differing only in whether the identifier points out or in, and the shipped carry-closure form already points in and already says `rootId`. **What is left is genuinely open and correctly bounded:** whether Room/Character hosts get the root too, which is AB-12's separable second bet, pressured but not settled by PQ-13 |
| AB-35 --- `positionCache` derivation, invalidation, and the never-a-second-truth rule | Not started --- invariant proposed, sub-questions open |
| Containment/extent separability --- can one composition be both (ship's rigging)? | **Answered 2026-08-06 by the wagon-in-a-doorway case: yes, and routinely.** Spanning and containment are independent axes. No longer a premise anything rests on |
| AB-25 --- H1's critical path and its live rollback trigger; now widened to admit characters as interior nodes | Not started; run after the character-held-end case, which feeds it directly |
| **AB-5 partially resolved --- minting is a planner-emitted compile-layer op; visibility is invalidation-driven refresh** | **Done (2026-08-10).** Both halves the row asks. **Mechanism:** an event, emitted as an abstract op in the `compilePositionKernelOp` family, not a derivation and not a presentation-time inference; footprint cleared (mint from the planner's own parts, dissolve from the port list). **Visibility:** player-visible by design in three named cases, always as a *consequence of a committed write* --- which is what keeps it inside AB-9's guardrail while making the guardrail structural rather than a remembered rule. **Filed outward:** AB-9 gains an enforcer and owes C8's carve-out wording; AB-11's cheap answer restored for lifecycle ops on H1-independent grounds; AB-18's convergence requirement gains its first mechanism; AB-38's lean toward (a) strengthened. **Found by tracing rather than assumed:** affordance invalidation reasons are a closed union keyed on object *existence*, so a purely graph-shaped mint invalidates nothing --- the `OBJECT#`-identity fork. **Open:** recognition's emitter, that fork, and AB-9's wording |
| **AB-5's remaining two forks closed, and the op re-framed as *interpose between A and B*** | **Done (2026-08-10).** **`OBJECT#` identity:** improvisation two-step --- a whole takes an `OBJECT#` because it **is** an object (C11 roles-not-kinds), which vindicates AB-0's no-type lock rather than straining it; gains embedding-findability (C3's complaint) and crisps AB-41's ephemeral/durable line. **Recognition's emitter:** its own producer, with P4's heat as a plausible *trigger* rather than a rival; AB-20's asymmetry then lands wholly at the producer and the kernel never learns which trigger fired. **The re-framing is the substantive change, and it corrected a misleading parallel this plan had already half-adopted:** minting is not object spawn (`transferMembership`'s pure-add, nothing pre-existed) --- everything involved already exists, and the op **interposes a level between a host A and a subset B of its members**. **Three obligations shrank as a result**, and are recorded at their corrected size rather than the drafted one: establish/dissolve are exact inverses (AB-17's ratchet cannot form); *release, do not delete* is structural because dissolve is a transfer; and the failed-mint residue is a stray name row over an unchanged world, not an orphan. **The real work relocated to the planning/compilation pathway (AB-11's second question).** **Open:** AB-9's carve-out wording, plus a footprint check --- mint's `toHostId` is a host that does not exist at plan time |
| **AB-9's perception carve-out committed; AB-5 closed** | **Done (2026-08-10).** **The carve-out, committed rather than admitted as an exception:** *a perception command actively seeking detail beyond what is currently represented, above or below the coarseness now held, may mint the level it seeks.* **The line is passive rendering versus active seeking** --- ordinary description still mints nothing, ever --- stated in terms of **player intent** because that is what the parse can see and a call-site rule would need re-deriving at every read path. **Bidirectional deliberately:** a downward-only licence would install the decompose-but-not-compose asymmetry AB-0's departure 3 exists to prevent. **It unifies AB-5's flashlight and chef's-knife cases into one rule** --- the strongest evidence it is drawn at a joint rather than fitted to [C8](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md#c8-the-flashlight-that-vanished-when-someone-looked-at-it) --- and is **naturally idempotent**, so repeated seeking cannot fork the world. **Cost recorded rather than minimized:** AB-9's scope control genuinely weakens; what is given up is a clean *never*, bounded on three axes. **Gap held open on purpose:** the rule licenses *seeking*, not *what may be found* --- AB-19 and the P5 licence surface --- and it does **not** inherit AB-20's *the-player-said-it-so-there-is-one*, because a question is not an assertion. **AB-5 closes** with two verifications relocated to Parked extension points on AB-40(a)'s precedent (`establishRelation` carrying `PartOf`; a footprint entry for a not-yet-existent host), and an internal inconsistency corrected --- AB-40(a)'s interior addressing is **not** needed for an ordinary mint |

## Lifecycle

**Three files dispose together** --- this plan, [`AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md`](AGENT.abstractionLayers.corpus.planning.md), and [`AGENT.abstractionLayers.discussion.planning.md`](AGENT.abstractionLayers.discussion.planning.md). The split is an organizational convenience, not three lifecycles. **One asymmetry to decide at disposal rather than now:** the corpus is the only part with a plausible claim to outliving the initiative --- worked examples that a later reader could use to test a *further* change --- so when this plan is retired, ask whether the corpus graduates somewhere durable instead of being deleted with the rest.

Dispose once the design settles and its lasting content lives in [`positions/AGENT.concepts.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.concepts.md) / [`AGENT.contract.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.contract.md) / [`AGENT.implementation.md`](../../../../../lambda/ephemera/dataSource/positions/AGENT.implementation.md) --- **unless** Phase 7 concludes this is a multi-iteration ladder, in which case this file converts to a standing index (same posture as [`AGENT.objectManipulationIterations.planning.md`](../actions/AGENT.objectManipulationIterations.planning.md)) and its lifecycle note is rewritten accordingly.
